pāppā mutal pāṭṭi vari


					pulavar ta. kōvēntaṉ





patippāṇṭu kuṟippilli





12 jūṉ, 2024 aṉṟu vikkimūlattil iruntu pativiṟakkappaṭṭatu

patippurimi aṟṟatu

inta ākkattuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavarkaḷ, ulakaḷaḷāviya potup payaṉpāṭṭukku eṉa patippurimic caṭṭattukku uṭpaṭṭu, taṅkaḷ aṉittup patippurimikaḷiyum viṭuvittuḷḷaṉar.

nīṅkaḷ ivvākkattip paṭiyeṭukkalām; mēmpaṭuttalām; pakiralām; vēṟu vaṭivamāka māṟṟalām; vaṇikap payaṉkaḷum aṭiyalām. ivaṟṟukku nīṅkaḷ opputal ētum kōrat tēviyilli.

***

pāppā mutal pāṭṭi vari





ta. kōvēntaṉ





vikṉēṣ veḷiyīṭu

19, niyū kālaṉi, jōciyar teru,

nuṅkampākkam, ceṉṉi - 600 034.





﻿patippuri

'talippik koṇṭu nūlik kaṇittu viṭakkūṭātu kaṇittu viṭa muṭiyātu’ eṉpataṟku innūl oru cāṉṟāta viḷakkukiṟatu.

karppattil toṭaṅki kaṭimuṭivukku iṭippaṭṭa vaḷarccip paruvaṅkaḷiṉ vaḷarcci niliyi oṭṭi ēṟpaṭum nōykaḷiyum, ataṟkuriya kāraṇa kāriyaṅkaḷiyum, pala talippukaḷil viḷakkamāka, cikiṭci muṟiyum, maruntu vakiyum, mikac ciṟappāka eṭuttuk kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu ciṟappākum.

tali toṭarpāṉa kaṇ, kātu, mūkku,vāy paṟṟiya nōykaḷukkum, mārpu, irutayam, nūliyīral, kuṭal, tōl eṉṉum neñcakam uḷḷiṭṭa pakutip pātippukaḷukkum, kāl, ki, kaḻuttu, tōḷ, iṭuppu eṉṟa aci nili toṭarpāṉa ceyalpaṭu nilic ciramam paṟṟiya iyakkac cikkalkaḷ, elumputtēyvu, elumpu muṟivu, naramputtaḷarcci pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟkum, karppattoṭakka mutal ēṟpaṭum uṭal māṟṟaṅkaḷ, ataṉāl ēṟpaṭum cōrvu, campantappaṭṭa taṉmikaḷi viḷakki, atu toṭarpākap piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭiya muṟikaḷ, uṭkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya uṇavu, maruntu vakikaḷum eṭuttu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.



﻿kuḻanti vaḷarppil koḷḷavēṇṭiya, kāṭṭa vēṇṭiya akkari, uṇavu, cikicci eṉavum virivāka eṭutturikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

peṇkaḷ paṟṟiya cantēkaṅkaḷukku, ṭākṭar kamalā celvarācu avarkaḷiṉ paltiṟappaṭṭa kēḷvikaḷukkuriya maruttuva viḷakkaṅkaḷum, nōy aṟikuṟi, ataṟkuriya cikiṭcikaḷum mikat teḷivākavē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

ovvoru vīṭṭilum kuṭumpattilum irukka vēṇṭiya oppaṟṟa, ariya maruttuva viḷakka nūlākum itu.

palvakiyāṉa nōykaḷ, kuḻantippēṟu, vaḷarcci, parāmarippu, pātukāppu, ūṭṭaccattu, eṉa mikat teḷivākavē viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

potuvāka uṭaliyum, uṟuppukaḷiyum, pātukāttu, nōyaṟṟa vāḻvu vāḻa vaki kūṟum ”pāppā mutal pāṭṭi vari” nalla payaṉtarum maruttuva nūlākum.

﻿pāppā mutal pāṭṭi vari



* * *





kuḻanti

ovvoru kuḻantiyum piṟakkum pōtu, uṭalilum uḷḷattilum paḻutillātatākavum nalamuṭiyatākavum piṟakka vēṇṭum. appoḻutē atu vaḷarntu nalla kuṭiyāka iyalum. kuḻanti nalla vitamākap piṟakkumāṟu ceyvataṟkāka, ik kālattil karuppamuṟṟa peṇkaḷiṉ nalattip pātukākka vēṇṭiya muyaṟcikaḷ mēṟ koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. piṟanta piṟaku ēṟpaṭuvatik kāṭṭilum, piṟakkum muṉṉarē kuḻantiyiṉ uṭal vaḷarcciyil māṟutalkaḷ mikutiyāka ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl karppiṇikaḷ, aṭikkaṭi maruttuvariṭam ceṉṟu uṭalniliyip paricōtittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. maruttuvarkaḷ paricōtaṉi ceytu, avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya uṇavut tiṭṭatti viḷakkik kūṟuvārkaḷ. ētēṉum nōy kāṇiṉ, ati nīkkuvārkaḷ. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, kiranti nōy (syphils) uḷḷa peṇkaḷ karuppamuṟṟa toṭakkattilēyē takkavāṟu cikicci ceytukoṇṭāl tāṉ kuḻanti nalamākap piṟakkum. illiyeṉil karuccitivu uṇṭākum. allatu iṟantu piṟakkum. allatu kuḻanti ﻿piṟantu cila vāraṅkaḷā vataṟkuḷ iṟantu viṭum. veṭṭi nōykaḷuḷḷa peṇkaḷ cikicci ceytu kuṇappaṭuttik koḷḷā viṭṭāl, piṟakkum kuḻantikkut toṟṟu uṇṭāki, ataṉ kaṇkaḷ kuruṭāka ētuvuṇṭu.

karppiṇi cattuḷḷa cīruṇavu uṇṇavēṇṭum. maruttuvar tavirkkumpaṭi kuṟippiṭum cilavaki uṇavukaḷi uṇṇalākātu. karppiṇikaḷ viṭṭamiṉkaḷiyum, mīṉ eṇṇiy pōṉṟa ciṟappu uṇavukaḷiyum uṇṇavēṇṭum eṉṟu maruttuvar kūṟuvar. mēlum avarkaḷ cukātāra vitiyiṉpaṭiyē oḻukavēṇṭum. ivvāṟu naṭantukoṇṭāl kuḻanti nallavitamākap piṟakkum.

kuḻanti piṟantatum, mutirntavarkaḷiṉiṉṟum pala viṣayaṅkaḷil māṟupaṭṭirukkum. kuḻantiyiṉ tali ataṉ uṭampiṉ uyarattil, nālil oru pākamākavum, mutirntavar taliyil ēḻil oru pākamākavumirukkum. kuḻantiyiṉ taliyiṉ cuṟṟaḷavu mārpiṉ cuṟṟaḷavilum periyatāyirukkum. kālkaḷ kikaḷilum, nīḷam kuṟintu kāṇum. mutirntavar oru nimiṣattil 20 taṭavi mūcciḻuppar. āṉāl kuḻanti 55 taṭavi mūcciḻukkum. piṟanta kuḻanti potuvāka 18-22 aṅkula nīḷamum, 6-8 irāttal eṭiyumuḷḷatāyirukkum; peṇ kuḻanti āṇ kuḻantiyiṉ uyarattilum eṭiyilum, ciṟitu kuṟivākavēyirukkum. kuḻanti piṟantatum uṭkāra vittāl, ataṉ uyaram 14 aṅkulamirukkum. kuḻantiyiṉ mārpuc cuṟṟaḷavu cu. 13 1/2 aṅkulamum, taliyiṉ cuṟṟaḷavu cu.14 aṅkulamumirukkum. vayiṟṟiṉ cuṟṟaḷavu taliyiṉaḷavāka irukkum.

kuḻantiyiṉ tōl, oruvita veṇpiciṉ pōṉṟa poruḷāl mūṭappaṭṭirukkum. kuḻantiyik kuḷippāṭṭiya piṉṉum, apporuḷ taṅkiyirukkumāyiṉ, ciṟitu nalleṇṇey taṭavit tuṇiyāl metuvākat tuṭittu nīkka vēṇṭum. kuḻanti piṟantavuṭaṉ pārttāl ataṉ irippi vayiṟṟuḷ cāyvāka irukkum. ataṉāl eḷitil vānti ēṟpaṭakkūṭum. kuḻantiyiṉ vāyil umiḻ nīr ūṟuvatilli. ataṉāl, kuḻantiyāl sṭārccic cerittukkoḷḷa muṭiyātu. paṟkaḷ muḷikkum pōtē umiḻnīr curakkat toṭaṅkum.

kuḻantiyiṉ nuriyīral, ciṟitu ciṟitāka virintu, 6 vayatilēyē muḻu virivu peṟukiṉṟatu. kuḻantiyiṉ mūḷi 6 vayatuvari virivākap parukkiṉṟatu. uccap parumaṉ vaḷarcci aṭivatu mutal āṇṭilākum. āṟāvatu āṇṭukkup piṉ metuvākavē paruttu varum. kuḻantiyiṉ mūḷiyil maṭippukkaḷ (Convolutions) nāḷaṭivilēyē uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa.

piṟantavuṭaṉ, atu cīraṇam, kaḻivu, iratta ōṭṭam, mūccu ākiya toḻilkaḷ naṭappataṟku ēṟṟavaṇṇamirunta pōtilum, ataṉ aimpoṟikaḷ uṭaṉēyē toḻiṟpaṭak kūṭiyaṉavāka iruppatilli. cila kālam ceṉṟa piṉṉarē, atu pārkavum, kēṭkavum, mukaravum, cuvikkavum kūṭiyatāka ākum.

kuḻantikku mutalil karumi, veṇmi tavira vēṟu niṟavēṟupāṭu teriyātu; iraṇṭu mūṉṟu mātam kaḻittē teriyum. atu piṟantatum, aḻavum, koṭṭāvi viṭavum vallatu. āṉāl pacikkiṟatu eṉṟum, uṟakkam varukiṟatu eṉṟum terintu koḷḷātu. ataṉ paciyum, uṟakkamum, veṟum maṟiviṉikaḷālēyē naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa.

kuḻanti piṟantavuṭaṉ ataṉ veppanili mutirntavaruṭiya veppa niliyākiya 98.6 pā.. āka irātu. oruvāram ceṉṟapiṉṉarē, innili aṭiyum. kuḻanti piṟantapōtu veyiṟkālamāyiṉ, kuḻantikku atikamāṉa tuṇikaḷip pōrtti, atika veppamaṭiyac ceyyakkūṭātu. appaṭic ceytāl, icivu (Convulsion) ēṟpaṭalām.

kuḻantiyiṉ malam karuppacci nīr pōlirukkum. uṇavu uṇṇat toṭaṅkiya piṉṉarē atu paḻuppu niṟamākavum kaṭṭiyākavum ākum.

kuḻanti piṟantatum koppūḻk koṭiyil oru pakuti kuḻantiyiṭamirukkumāṟu viṭṭu viṭṭu, aṟuttu maruntu vittuk kaṭṭuvar. eñciyuḷḷa pakuti, cātāraṇamāka oru vārattil curuṅkip piṉ viḻuntuviṭum. piṟanta kuḻantiyip paṭukka vittāl, mutirntavarpōl kāli nīṭṭip paṭukka ataṉāl muṭiyātu. atu taliyi oru pakkamākat tiruppiyum, kālkaḷi maṭakkiyum, kikaḷik kaḻuttiṉiṭiyē vittuk koṇṭum paṭukkum. atāvatu tāyiṉ karuppiyil irunta niliyilēyē iruntukoḷkiṟatu.

kuḻanti piṟanta piṉ cila vāraṅkaḷ vari, perumpakutiyāṉa nēram uṟaṅkik koṇṭē irukkum. viḻittirukkum poḻutil iṭiviṭāmal kālkaḷi āṭṭikkoṇṭē irukkum.

kuḻantiyiṉ elumpukaḷ mirutuvākavum vaḷiyak kūṭiyaṉavākavum irukkum. maṇṭiyōṭṭup pakutikaḷ iṇinta iṭattil, āṟiṭaṅkaḷ mirutuvāka irukkum. ataṉāl avaṟṟukku ūṟu ēṟpaṭātavāṟu kavaṉittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.

kuḻantiyiṉ uccit taliyil, iraṇṭiṭaṅkaḷil toṭṭāl, atika mirutuvāyirukkum. avaṟṟil (Soft spots) oṉṟu peritāyum, oṉṟu ciṟiyatāyumirukkum. ﻿ciṟiyatu āṟu mātamākumpōtu naṉṟāka mūṭiviṭum. periyatu 12 mātamākum vari naṉṟāka mūṭāmalirukkum.

kuḻanti tāy vayiṟṟil evvaḷavu virivāka vaḷarntatō, avvaḷavu virivākap piṟanta piṉṉar vaḷarvatilli. kuḻanti piṟanta oru vāra kālattil, ataṉ eṭiyil 1/2 irāttal kuṟiyum. ēṉeṉil, tāyiṭam ūṟum pālil toṭakkattil atika ūṭṭamirukkātu. atip pāl eṉṟa kūṟāmal, cīmpāl (Colostrum) eṉṟu kūṟuvar. atu malamiḷakkiyākavē (Laxative) payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. piṟaku tāyppāl ūṭṭa muṭiyatākum. ataṉāl iraṇṭu vāra iṟutiyil kuḻantiyiṉ eṭi piṟantapoḻutu irunta nilikku vantu piṉ ēṟivarum. kuḻantiyiṉ eṭi, piṟantavuṭaṉ iruppatu pōl, atu āṟumātam ceṉṟatum, iraṇṭu maṭaṅkākavumākum. ōrāṇṭuk kuḻantiyiṉ carācari eṭi 21 irāttal, carācari uyaram 30 aṅkulam. iraṇṭu vayatil eṭi 26 irāttalum, uyaram 33 aṅkulamirukkum.

mutal āṟumātattil uṟuppukkaḷiṉ vaḷarcci oṉṟu pōl naṭipeṟum.ataṉ piṉ 18 mātattil kālkaḷ virivākavum, taliyum, naṭuvuṭalum metuvākavum vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭu vayatuk kuḻantiyiṉ taliyiṉ uyaram, muḻu uṭaliṉ uyarattil aintil oṉṟāyirukkum.

kuḻanti piṟantatum ataṉ itayam evvaḷavu parumaṉāyirukkumō atupōl mutal āṇṭil iraṇṭu maṭaṅkāka ākum. iraṇṭām āṇṭil itayam metuvāka vaḷarum. uṭal muḻuvatum parumaṉāvatilum, iraṇṭu maṭaṅkukaḷākat tacikaḷ parumaṉaṭikiṉṟaṉa.

kuḻanti piṟantavuṭaṉ atik tūkkip piṭittāl, tali nimirntu niṟkātu paṭukkavittāl, tali vitta iṭattilēyē irukkum. nāṉku mātam ākumpōtu, kuḻanti kaviḻntu paṭukkavum, taliyit tūkki nēril pārkkavum kūṭiyatāyirukkum. taliyi iru pakkaṅkaḷilum tiruppavum muṭiyum. uṭkāra vittāl muṉpakkamākac cāyntuviṭum, nimira muṭiyātu. oṉpatu mātam ākumpōtē atu nimirntu uṭkāra muṭiyum. ōr āṇṭu ākiya piṉṉarē, atu paṭukkavum, eḻuntu uṭkāravum muṭiyum. inta nili iruntāl tāṉ naṭakkum nili varum.

kuḻanti puṉṉaki ceyvatu, 4-6 vāraṅkaḷ kaḻinta piṉṉarē. ataṉ piṉ cila vāraṅkaḷ ceṉṟatum, kuḻanti ki kālkaḷik koṇṭu viḷiyāṭavum, naṇpar, ayalār vēṟupāṭu teriyavum, uṇavu kaṇṭatum, vāyit tiṟakkavum, paṭukkiyil puraḷavum kūṭum. 6-8 mātamāṉatum, atu nimirntu uṭkārak kūṭiyatāka irukkum. kuḻanti mutalil kikaḷiyum muḻaṅkālkaḷiyum ūṉṟi niṉṟu, muṉṉum piṉṉumāka aciyum. ippōtu kālkaḷ uṭaliṉum virivāka vaḷarum. inta niliyil atu kālkaḷi iḻuttuk koṇṭu nakarat toṭaṅkum. piṉṉarē tavaḻa muṭiyum. appōtu aṭikkaṭi uṭkāra vittup paḻakkiṉāl, atu ciṟitu ciṟitākat tavaḻat toṭaṅkum. ataṉ piṉ niṟkavum, iṟutiyil naṭakkavum āṟṟaluṟum. ōrāṇṭu āṉa piṟakutāṉ, piṟar utaviyiṉṟi naṭakkum. kuḻantikkut takka uṇavu ūṭṭivantāl, atu virivil naṭakkat toṭaṅkiṉālum, ataṉ kāl elumpukaḷ vaḷiyā.

kuḻanti piṟanta poḻutiliruntē aḻat toṭaṅkalām. kuḻanti 4-5 mātamākum vari atu aḻumpōtu kaṇkaḷil nīr kāṇappaṭamāṭṭātu. kuḻanti, mutal āṇṭilēyē ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷi vittu viḷiyāṭum. atik kiyil eṭuttu vīci eṟiya muṭiyum. kiyi nīṭṭiṉāl toṭak kūṭiya tolivil niṟam tīṭṭiya poruḷkaḷi vittu vantāl, kuḻanti kaṇkaḷiyum, kikaḷiyum toṭarpu paṭuttum vaḻakkatti aṟintukoḷḷum. āṟu mātam āṉapiṉ, atu kikkeṭṭum toliviluḷḷa poruḷkaḷi eṭukkap peru viralip payaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅkum. eti eṭuttālum, ati atu taṉ vāyil iṭum. ataṉāl, poruḷkaḷ tūyaṉavākavum, toṇṭiyil cikkāta aḷavuḷḷaṉavākavum iruttal vēṇṭum.

kuḻanti iraṇṭām āṇṭilum, kāli viriya vittē naṭakkum. appōtu atu kutikkavum, ōṭavum, ēṟavum muṭiyum. ciṟitu ciṟitākap pēcak kaṟṟakkoḷḷum. iraṇṭāṇṭu ceṉṟa kuḻantikku, ēṟakkuṟiya 250 coṟkaḷ teriyum.

kuḻanti, mutal oṉṟari āṇṭil, accam, ciṉam, veṟuppu ākiyavaṟṟik kāṭṭak kūṭum. makiḻcciyi aṟivikkac cirikkum, kūttāṭum. mutal āṇṭil kuḻantiyiṭam uṟaṅkutal, uṇṇutal, kaḻittal pōṉṟa ceyalkaḷic ceyyum paḻakkam uṇṭākum.

kuḻanti piṟakkumpōtu paṟkaḷ veḷiyē terivatilli. avi īṟukaḷiluḷḷa paṟpikaḷil (Dental sacs) maṟintuḷḷaṉa.

mutal āṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil pāl paṟkaḷ (Milkteeth) veḷiyē muḷikkat toṭaṅkum. cila kuḻantikaḷukku, nāṭkaḷittu muḷikkalām. appaṭi muḷippatil tavaṟu illi. mottam 20 pāl paṟkaḷum, 30 mātamāvataṟkuḷ muḷittuviṭum. āṟāvatu vayatil pāl paṟkaḷ viḻuntu, ﻿nilip paṟkaḷ (Permanent teeth) muḷikkat toṭaṅki, āṇṭukku 4 paṟkaḷ vītam muḷittu, 28 nilippaṟkaḷ 12–13 vayatil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mītip paṟkaḷ (Wisdom teeth ) 17-21 vayataḷavil muḷikkiṉṟaṉa. pārkka : kuḻanti uḷaviyal; kuḻanti vaḷarppu; kuḻanti nōykaḷ.





kuḻantikaḷ tōṭṭappaḷḷi mayilāppūr

ippaḷḷi 1937 cepṭemparil toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. paṇṭiya kurukulak kalviyiṉ uṇmiyi oṭṭiyum, perumi peṟṟa aṟivāḷikaḷāṉa pālkahēp, purōpel (jermaṉi), pesṭalāji (cuviṭsarlāntu) māṉṭicōri (ittāli), ṭuyi (amerikkā) mutalāṉavarkaḷiṉ muṟikaḷi oṭṭiyum, intiyar paṇpāṭṭiṟku ottatāṉa kalvi iṅkē pōtikkappaṭukiṟatu. kuḻantikaḷ vīṭṭil paḻakuvatu pōlavē, āciriyarkaḷuṭaṉ payamiṉṟip paḻakukiṟārkaḷ. kuḻantikaḷ tuṇivuṭaṉum taṉṉampikkiyōṭum, poruḷkaḷi ārāyntu aṟintu koḷḷak kūṭiya muṟiyil ip paḷḷi naṭipeṟukiṟatu.

iraṇṭu mutal aintu vayatu variyuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷukkup pulaṉ payiṟci mukkiyamākiyāl, viḷiyāṭṭu muṟippaṭi upakaraṇaṅkaḷ, pommikaḷ koṇṭu payiṟci tarappaṭukiṟatu. kuḻantikaḷ poṟuppi uṇarvataṟkāṉa vāyppukkaḷ pala koṭukkappaṭuṉṟaṉa. camūkap paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷik kaṟpikkak kavaṉam celuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. ciṟu kuḻantikaḷ, ip paruvattil paṭikkavum, eḻutavum kaṟṟukkoḷvatilli. āṉāl, maṟṟac ciṟu kuḻantikaḷuṭaṉ varṇam tīṭṭiyum, vīṭu kaṭṭiyum, pāṭiyum, āṭiyum makiḻkiṉṟaṉar. kuḻantikaḷ eppoḻutum, taṉiyākavum, āciriyaruṭaṉum, iyaṟkik kuḻuviṉaruṭaṉum cērntu vēli ceyvatil ūkkam peṟukiṉṟaṉar.



﻿5-7 vayatuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷ, paṭikkavum, eḻutavum kūṭiya kāla aṭṭavaṇi tayārikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ici, nāṭṭiyam. kivēli, kākita vēli, paṭam varital ivikaḷ, kāliyilum, māliyilum, kaṭici nērattil naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 7-12 vayatuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷukkāka iṭinilip paḷḷiyil pāṭamum naṭattappaṭukiṟatu.

piruntāvaṉap payiṟcip pakuti eṉṟa pōtaṉā muṟippaḷḷi oṉṟu, kiṇṭar kārṭṭaṉ āciriyarkaḷit tayārikka itaṉuṭaṉ iṇikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. kiṇṭar kārṭṭaṉ pirivu, iṭinilip paḷḷip pirivu, piruntāvaṉap payiṟcip pakuti, ākiya mūṉṟum aracāṅkattāl aṅkīkarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivikaḷē allāmal tāymārkaḷukkuk kuḻantip parāmarippu vakuppukkaḷum naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.





kuḻanti nōykaḷ

vayatu vantavarkaḷukku varakkūṭiya pala nōykaḷ, kuḻantikaḷiyum paṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum, kuḻantikaḷukkeṉa varum cila taṉippaṭṭa nōykaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. ēṉeṉil uṭal vaḷarcci, uṟuppukkaḷiṉ amippu, nōy taṭukkum caktip poruḷ uṟpatti ākiyavaṟṟil kuḻantiyiṉ uṭal, vayatu vanta maṉitaruṭiya uṭaliṉiṉṟum mikavum māṟupāṭuṭiyatu.

mutirntavarkaḷukku varum pala nōykaḷ kuḻantikaḷip pātikkum pōtu, veḷiyē tōṉṟum kuṟikaḷ, cila māṟupāṭukaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. utāraṇamāka, cātāraṇa pēti nōyiṉāl vayatu vanta maṉitaṉiṉ uṭal nalam mikavum pātikkappaṭṭu viṭuvatilli. āṉāl atē nōy, oru ciṟu kuḻantiyi ari vēkattuṭaṉ pātittālum, ak kuḻantiyiṉ uṭalnalam mikavum keṭṭu, nōyiṉ ciṉṉam mikavum tīviramāka veḷiyē kāṇappaṭum. itaṉ kāraṇam; kuḻantip paruvattil mārpiṉmītu taci vaḷarcci kuṟivu; itayam muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭintu, oḻuṅkāṉa niliyi aṭivatilli; taliyelumpukaḷ oṉṟuṭaṉ oṉṟu kūṭātu, pirinta niliyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl nōyiṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷ, muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭiyāta uṟuppukkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē veḷiyē tōṉṟum pōtu, mikavum vēṟupāṭṭuṭaṉum, koṭumiyākavum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iraṇṭāvatākak kuḻantiyiṉ uṭalāṉatu, vaḷarccip paruvattil irukkiṉṟatu. purōṭṭīṉ, viṭṭamiṉkaḷ, uppukkaḷ mutaliyaṉa, mutirntavariṉ tēviyi viṭa atika aḷavil kuḻantiyiṉ uṭal vaḷarccikku vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷ uṇavākavē kuḻantiyiṉ uṭali aṭiya muṭiyum. pēti pōṉṟa nōy ēṟpaṭukiyil, kuḻanti iraṇṭuvita kaṣṭaṅkaḷukku uḷḷākiṉṟatu. aḷitta uṇavi ēṟṟukkoḷḷa muṭiyāmal, kuṭal acīraṇa niliyil iruppatu oṉṟu; uṭalil koñca nañcamirukkum cattup poruḷkaḷum, pēti vaḻiyē veḷiyēṟiviṭuvatu maṟṟoṉṟu. ataṉāl nālintu taṭavikaḷ pēti āṉatum, nāṭi palavīṉamaṭintu, kaṇkaḷ oḷi maṅki, uccikkuḻi paḷḷamākit, tōl curuṅkik kāyccaluṭaṉ kuḻanti mikavum palavīṉa niliyi aṭintuviṭukiṟatu. kuḻantikaḷukkeṉat taṉippaṭṭu varum cila nōykaḷukkuk kāraṇam kuḻantikaḷiṉ uṭalamippē tāṉ. ip paruvattil uṭal uṟuppukkaḷ vaḷarcciyaṭiyum niliyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. kaṇinōy, coṟi karappāṉ eṉṉum nōykaḷ vēkamāka vaḷarkiṉṟa elumpukaḷi eḷitil tākki, nōyi uṇṭākki viṭukiṉṟaṉa. kuḻantip paruvattil nōyit taṭukkum iyaṟkiyāṉa cakti mikavum kuṟivu. ataṉ ﻿kāraṇamākavum, cila nōykaḷ virivil kuḻantikaḷip pīṭittuk koḷkiṉṟaṉa.

nōykaḷ: kuḻanti nōykaḷ palavakippaṭṭavi. cila nōykaḷ, kuḻanti piṟappataṟku muntiyē aṭikōlat tuvaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷip pārāmpariya nōykaḷ eṉak kūṟuvārkaḷ. peṟṟōrkaḷiṭamiruntu kākki valippu (Epilepsy), mūḷimantam pōṉṟa nōykaḷum, mūḷi utaṭu (Hare lip) pōṉṟa piṟavik kōḷāṟukaḷum varakkūṭum. tamiḻ nāṭṭil kāṇappaṭum īral kulikkaṭṭi eṉappaṭum nōyum oru vakiyil pārampariyamāka varum nōyeṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.

ivvāṟu kuḻantikaḷukku varum nōykaḷit taṭuppatu eḷitu. maṇam ceytu koḷkiṉṟavarkaḷ, kākki valippu, pittiyam, mūḷimantam pōṉṟa nōykaḷ illātirukkiṉṟa kuṭumpamā eṉak kavaṉittu maṇam purintu koḷvatu nalam. kṣayam, kuṣṭam ivikaḷ uḷḷavarkaḷ nalla tiṟamiyāṉa cikicciyiṉāl muṟṟum kuṇamaṭintu viṭṭaṉar eṉat terinta piṉpu tāṉ maṇantu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

karuppiyil irukkiyil kuḻantikaḷukkuc cila nōykaḷ ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tāyiṉ irattam kirantip puṇ nōyiṉāl keṭṭiruntālum, tāy takunta cukātārap pātukāppukkaḷik kiyāḷaviṭṭālum, karuppa kālattil tāy ūṭṭam mikunta uṇavu uṭkoḷḷā viṭṭālum, kuḻantikaḷukkuc cila nōykaḷ ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷit taṭuppatum eḷitu. uṭal niliyi appōtikkappōtu ārāyntu, irattam keṭṭiruntāl, uṭaṉaṭiyākat takunta cikicci ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. karuppa kālattil maruttuvar ālōcaṉiyik kēṭṭu, uṭal nalap pātukāppukaḷi ārāyntu, nalla uṇavukaḷi uṭkoṇṭāl, kuḻantikaḷi in nōykaḷ pātikkātu kāppāṟṟa muṭiyum.

piracavikkum poḻutu, cila nōykaḷ kuḻantikaḷip pātikkiṉṟaṉa. tāyiṉ piracava uṟuppu, veṭṭi nōyiṉāl pātikkappaṭṭiruntāl, piṟakkum kuḻantiyiṉ kaṇkaḷi veṭṭinōyk kirumikaḷ tākki, oruvaki nōyi uṇṭu paṇṇukiṉṟaṉa. cila camayam piracavam mikat tuṉpamāki viṭukiṟatu. piracava camayattil āyutaṅkaḷik kiyāṇṭatiṉālum, nīṇṭa nēram tāyiṉ karuppiyil taṅka nērvatiṉālum, mūccu muṭṭal, taliyil kāyam, mūḷiyil irattap perukku pōṉṟa cila apāyaṅkaḷ, kuḻantikku ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷit taṭuppatum eḷitu. piracava camayattil takuntapaṭi kavaṉittāl, kuḻantikku inta apāyaṅkaḷ nērātu taṭukka muṭiyum.

kuḻanti piṟantavuṭaṉ, mutal nāṉku vāraṅkaḷ cila nōykaḷ ēṟpaṭakkūṭum. karuppiyiṉiṉṟu veḷiyē vantatiṉāl ēṟpaṭum oru putiya cūḻniliyic camāḷittuk koṇṭu, ataṟkut takkavaṇṇam, taṅkaḷic carippaṭuttik koḷḷak kuḻantiyiṉ uṭal uṟuppukkaḷ muyaṟci ceykiyila, im muyaṟciyil ciṟitu kuṟipāṭu ēṟpaṭumākil, kuḻanti caṭṭeṉa nōykaḷukku ilakkāki viṭukiṉṟatu. muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭiyātu, kuṟi mātattilē piṟakkum kuḻantikaḷi nōykaḷ eḷitākap pīṭittuk koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟum, piṟanta, mutal mātattil nōyiṉāl iṟakkum kuḻantikaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyil pāti, kuṟi mātattil piṟanta kuḻantikaḷ tām eṉak kaṇakkiṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ.

mutal iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷil kuḻantiyiṉ uṭal virivāka vaḷarcciyaṭiyum. uṭal naliviṉālum, ūṭṭak kuṟiviṉālum, irumal, caḷi pōṉṟa nōykaḷ, inta vayatil eḷitil ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. potuvākat toṟṟu nōykaḷ intap paruvattil kuḻantikaḷi eḷitil pīṭikkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl cila kuṟippiṭṭa nōykaḷit taṭukkum caktip poruḷkaḷ mutal nāṉku allatu āṟu mātaṅkaḷ kuḻantiyiṉ irattattil atika aḷavil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ataṉ kāraṇamākat taṭṭammi (Meales), iḷampiḷḷi (Diphtherial), vātam pōṉṟa kuṟippiṭṭa nōykaḷ, mutal vayatil atikam varuvatilli. āṉāl, potuvāka, ammi, nimōṉiyā, iṉpuḷuyaṉcā pōṉṟa toṟṟu nōykaḷ ipparuvattil virivil tākkukiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭām mātattiliruntu mutal vayatu vari kuḻantikaḷ nōyiṉāl iṟakkum eṇṇikki, mutal mātattil nōyiṉāl maraṇamaṭiyum eṇṇikkiyiviṭak kuṟivu. nōyiṉāl iṟakkum kuḻantikaḷil āṇ kuḻantikaḷ tāṉ atikam. kuḷirkālattil piṟakkum kuḻantikaḷilum, maraṇa eṇṇikki atikam. nōykaḷukkum, avaṟṟāl ēṟpaṭum maraṇattiṟkum vaṟumi ōraḷavu kāraṇamākiṟatu.

paḷḷip paruvattiṟku munti, atāvatu iraṇṭu vayatiliruntu aintu vari, uḷḷa kuḻantikaḷukku varum nōykaḷ, mutal vayatik kāṭṭilum mikavum kuṟivu. ip paruvattil nōykaḷit taṭukkum caktip poruḷkaḷ uṭalil uṇṭāki, irattattil kalantu, etirkkat tuvaṅkiviṭuvatiṉāl pala nōykaḷ kuḻantikaḷi aṇṭuvatilli. maṟṟip paruvattil pōl inta vayatil, caḷi, irumal pōṉṟa nōykaḷ kuḻantikaḷip pātikkalām. kṣayam, kuṭalvālaḻaṟci pōṉṟa nōykaḷ tām ipparuvattuk kuḻantikaḷiṉ maraṇattiṟkum, peru vāriyāṉa kāraṇamāka amikiṉṟaṉa. ip paruvattuk kuḻantikaḷ, nōykaḷukku ilakkātalik kāṭṭilum, tī vaṇṭikaḷiṉāl ēṟpaṭum vipattukkaḷukku atikam ilakkākiṉṟaṉar.

paḷḷip paruvamāṉa, aintiliruntu patiṉāṉku vayatu vari uḷḷa kuḻankiḷi, nōykaḷum, vipattukkaḷum cari camamāṉa aḷavil pātikkiṉṟaṉa. inta vayatil mukkiyamāka muṭakkuk kāyccal (Dengue) kuḻantikaḷi atikam pīṭikkiṉṟatu, peṉiciliṉ, calpāṉilamiṭu pōṉṟa putiya nōy taṭukkum maruntukaḷ pala irukkum ik kālattil, ip paruvattil, nimōṉiyā, ṭipāyṭu mutaliya nōykaḷiṉāl, kuḻantikaḷ iṟattal mikavum kuṟintu varukiṉṟatu. ip paruvattil kuṭalvālaḻaṟci ēṟpaṭalām.

kaṇvali : tāyiṉ piracava pākattil veṭṭi nōy ēṟpaṭṭiruntāl, ataṉ vaḻiyē veḷiyēṟum kuḻantiyiṉ kaṇkaḷi, in nōy paṟṟukiṟatu. itit tavira, sṭrepṭōkākkas, sṭepilōkākkas, pācilas kōli, toṇṭiyaṭippāṉ mutaliya nōyk kirumikaḷum piṟanta kuḻantiyiṉ kaṇkaḷit tākkik kaṇvali uṇṭākkakkūṭum.

nōykuṟi : piṟanta irupattu nāṉku allatu nāṟpatteṭṭu maṇinēram ceṉṟapiṉ, kuḻantiyiṉ imikaḷ ilēcākac civantu, taṭittuk kāṇappaṭum; nīr kaciyum. imikaḷiṉ vīkkam atikarittatum, kaṇṇiṉ orattil puḷicci kaṭṭik koṇṭu imikaḷit tiṟakka muṭiyātu oṭṭik koḷḷac ceyyum. in nōy mutalil oru kaṇṇil toṭaṅki, maṟṟiya kaṇṇiṟkum paraviviṭum.

cikicci : kavaṉikkātu acaṭṭi ceytāl, innōy, pārviyip pātittu viṭum. iti varātu taṭuppatē nallatu. piṟantavuṭaṉ kuḻantiyiṉ kaṇkaḷi pōrik amilam karitta taṇṇīrāl pañciṉāl kaḻuvi, veḷḷi niṭrēṭṭuk karicali ūṟṟiṉāl innōy ēṟpaṭātu taṭuttuviṭalām. nōy vantuviṭṭāl, kaṇ maruttuvar utaviyi nāṭuvatu nalam. in nōykku ikkālattil calpāṉilamiṭu, peṉiciliṉ ṭemiciṉ pōṉṟa maruntukaḷ payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

kāmāli : piṟanta mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku nāḷukku mēl kuḻantiyiṉ uṭal mītu ilēcāṉa mañcaḷ niṟam kāṇappaṭuvatuṇṭu. itaṟkuk kāraṇam, kuḻantiyiṉ uṭalil tēvikku mīṟiya aḷavil civappu iratta aṇukkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. vēṇṭappaṭātavikaḷi, iyaṟki pōkkiviṭum poḻutu, avikaḷiṉiṉṟu veḷippaṭum mañcaḷ niṟapporuḷ, uṭaliṉ mītu ilēcāṉa oru mañcaḷ niṟatti uṇṭupaṇṇi viṭukiṉṟatu. āṉāl cila vēḷikaḷil, tōl maṭṭummiṉṟik, kaṇṇiṉ uṭpuṟamum ciṟu nīrum mañcaḷ niṟamākak kāṇappaṭum; malam kaḷi maṇṇip pōl veṇṇiṟattil kāṇappaṭum. ittuṭaṉ kāyccalum kuṟivākavō, atikamākavō irukkum. appōtu itik kāmāli nōy eṉpar. ciṟu nīrum, malamum, itaṉāl pātikkappaṭuvatilli. itu oru taṉippaṭṭa nōy alla. evvita cikicciyumiṉṟi nālintu tiṉaṅkaḷil mañcaḷ niṟam maṟintuviṭum. ivvāṟu piṟanta kuḻantikku varum mañcaḷ kāmālikkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ piṉ varupavi : cīraṇak kōḷāṟiṉāl pitta nīrk kōḷaṅkaḷiṉ uṭpuṟamuḷḷa melliya tōlil vīkkam ēṟpaṭṭup pitta nīrt tēkkam eṟpaṭuvatu oru vitattil nōykkuk kāraṇam pitta nīrk kuḻāy piṟaviyilēyē kōḷāṟāka iruntālum, kāmāli nōy ēṟpaṭak kāraṇamākiṉṟatu. koppūḻk koṭiyil cīḻ kaṭṭikkoṇṭu, koppūḻ vaḻiyē naccuk kirumikaḷ uḷḷē ceṉṟu, kallīralip pātippatālum kāmāli ēṟpaṭalām. itit tavira cila kuḻantikaḷukkup pāram pariyamākavē oruvitak kāmāli nōy ēṟpaṭakkūṭum.

cikicci : pārampariyamāka varum kāmāli nōyum, piṟaviyiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa kōḷāṟṟiṉ payaṉākap pitta nīrk kuḻāyil ēṟpaṭṭa kāmāli nōyum, perumpālum mikavum koṭiyavi; cikicci ceyyum muṉpē, kuḻantiyiṉ uyirukkuk kēṭu viḷivikkum vakiyic cērntavi. acīraṇattiṉāl ēṟpaṭum kāmāliyit takka cikicciyāl kuṇappaṭutta muṭiyum. oru tēkkaraṇṭi aḷavu āmaṇakkeṇṇey pētikkuc koṭuttāl, acīraṇam cariyāki, nōyum caṭṭeṉak kuṇamaṭintu viṭukiṟatu. pārkka : kāmāli.

vāyppūṭṭu nōy: (Tetanus) apūrvamāṉatoru nōy. piracava camayattil takunta maruttuva utavi illāviṭil, kuḻantikaḷukku ēṟpaṭalām. koppūḻk koṭiyi aṟukkak kiyāḷum kattarikkōl tūymi yāka illāviṭiṉum, koppūḻk koṭiyil uḷḷa kāyatti naṉṟāka mūṭi, maruntu vittuk kaṭṭip pātukākkāmal kirumikaḷ eḷitāka vantu kuṭikoḷḷumāṟu acaṭṭikayākap puṟakkaṇittu viṭṭālum, vāyppūṭṭu eṉṉum nōy ēṟpaṭalām. iti uṭaṉē kavaṉikkāviṭil, uyirukku apāyam ēṟpaṭum.

nōykkirumi : piṟanta ēḻu allatu pattu nāṭkaḷukkup piṟaku, kuḻanti vāyit tiṟakka muṭiyāmal, mūccuviṭac ciramappaṭṭuk kiyiyum, kāliyum iḻuttuk koṇṭu valippiṉāl ciramappaṭum. tāṭi, kaḻuttu, mārpu, vayiṟu ivikaḷ mītu uḷḷa taci mikavum kaṭiṉamākak kikkut teṉpaṭum.



cikikci : vāyppūṭṭu nōy etir maruntu (Antetanus) eṉappaṭum marunti ūcimūlam celutti, nōyik kaṇṭikka vēṇṭum. valippu varātu taṭukka, purōmiṭukaḷ pōṉṟa maruntukaḷi ūci kutta vēṇṭum. inta nōy vanta kuḻantikaḷi maruttuvac cāliyil vittuc cikicci ceyvatutāṉ nalam.

iḻuppu : piṟanta kuḻantikkum iḻuppu nōy ēṟpaṭuvatuṇṭu. nīṇṭa kālam karuppiyil taṅkiyiruntatiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa mūccaṭippu (Asphyxia), kāyccal, acīraṇam, piṟavik kōḷāṟukaḷ mutaliyavikaḷāl itu ēṟpaṭalām. pārkka : iḻuppu

irattap perukku (Haemorrhage) koppūḻk koṭi peṇ uṟuppu, kuṭal, tōl, mulikkāmpu ākiya uṟuppukkaḷiṉiṉṟum irattapperukku veḷippaṭak kūṭum. koppuḻk koṭiyiṉiṉṟu ēṟpaṭum irattak kacivu, iraṇṭu vakippaṭṭatu. koṭiyi naṉṟāka iṟukkik kaṭṭātatiṉāl, kuḻanti piṟanta oru maṇi nērattil koppūḻiliruntu irattam kacivatu oru vaki. iti uṭaṉē taṭukka iyalum. iṟukkamākak koppuḻk koṭi mītu iṉṉoru kaṭṭup pōṭṭuviṭṭāl itu niṉṟuviṭum. koppūḻkkoṭi vāyil kāṇappaṭum raṇattil naccuk kirumikaḷ kuṭikoṇṭu cīḻ kaṭṭikkoḷvatiṉāl, koppūḻil irattak kacivu ēṟpaṭuvatu maṟṟoru vaki. mutal vakiyiviṭa, itu mikavum apāyamāṉatu, koppūḻkkoṭi pirinta ēḻāvatu, eṭṭavātu nāḷ, koppūḻiliruntu irattam virivākak kacintu vantāl uṭaṉē maruttuva utaviyi nāṭuvatu nalam. ēṉeṉil, cila camayam inta irattak kacivi, niṟutta koppūḻic cuṟṟilum iṟukkamākap piyic curukkuvatu pōl oru tiyal pōṭavum vēṇṭiyirukkum.



﻿peṇ uṟuppiliruntu irattak kacivu : piṟanta cila nāṭkaḷukkup piṟaku cila kuḻantikaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭum. tāyiṉ irattattil kāṇappaṭum īsṭṭiriṉ eṉkiṟa curappu, kuḻantiyiṉ irattattilum aḷavukku mīṟi vantu cērntu viṭuvatiṉāl, mātaviṭāyip pōṉṟu koñcam irattak kacivu peṇ uṟuppil ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. itu iraṇṭu nāṭkaḷil tāṉē niṉṟu viṭumātalāl taṉippaṭṭa cikicci tēviyilli.

kuṭalil irattak kacivu : itu malattiṉ vaḻiyētāṉ teriyavarum. piṟantu mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku nāṭkaḷukkup piṟaku, kuḻanti kaḻikkum malam, karuniṟamāka kāṇappaṭum. cila camayam vāntiyilum irattam veḷippaṭum. intak kuḻantikaḷukku ittuṭaṉ iḻuppum vantuviṭalām. perumpālum, kirantippuṇ nōy uḷḷa peṟṟōrkkup piṟakkum kuḻantikaḷtām in nōykku uṭpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

cikikci : takka cikicci ceyyāviṭil maraṇam uṇṭākum. ūṭṭum uṇavi niṟuttiviṭṭu, kuḷukkōs nīr maṭṭum koṭukka vēṇṭum. irattam atikam veḷiyēṟiyiruntāl, patilukku irattam ūṭṭa vēṇṭum. ātalāl, intak kuḻantikaḷi maruttuvaccāliyil vittuccikicci ceyvatu nalam.

tōlil irattak kacivu : tōlil, aṅkaṅkē nīla niṟamāṉa puḷḷikaḷ pōl irattak kacivu kāṇappaṭum. naccuk kirumikaḷāl irattam keṭṭuppōṉāl, allatu pārampariyamāka irattatip pātikkakkūṭiya oru vaki nōyiṉāl tōlilē irattak kacivu ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. itu perumpālum apāyakaramāṉatoru nōy.

mulikkāmpil irattak kacivu : cilacamayam piṟanta kuḻantiyiṉ mulik kāmpukaḷiṉṟum koñcam irattam veḷiyē kacivatuṇṭu.



﻿cikicci : taṭittu vīṅkik kāṇappaṭum mārpiṉ mītu cūṭāṉa ottaṭam koṭukkavēṇṭum. kālciyam kuḷukkōṉēṭṭup poṭi oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu kireyiṉ aḷavil, oru nāḷikku mūṉṟu vēḷi, tēṉil kuḻittuc cāppiṭak koṭukka vēṇṭum.

cīraṇak karuvikaḷip pātikkum nōy : vāyppuṇ: vāyiṉ uṭpuṟattē amintuḷḷa melliya tōl, taṉakku iyaṟkiyāka uḷḷa nōyit taṭukkum cakti kuṟintu, nalivaṭiyum poḻutu palvēṟu naccuk kirumikaḷ atit tākki, vāyiṉ uṭpuṟattilum, utaṭukaḷiṉ ōrattilum ciṟu kuḻippuṇkaḷi uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa. vāyil puṇ uṇṭākak kāraṇam : 1. kuḻantikku aḷikkappaṭum uṇavil ‘ci’ viṭṭamiṉ kuṟivāka iruttal. 2. pal muḷikkum paruvattil, vayiṟu vīṅkik kaṉamāki, palavīṉamāki viṭutal. 3. potuvākap palavita nōyiṉāl kuḻantiyiṉ uṭal nalam paḻutupaṭṭiruttal. 4. kuṭalum, irippiyum acīraṇattiṉāl pātikkap paṭṭiruntal. 5. ṭipāyṭu, taṭṭammi pōṉṟa nōyiṉ pōtu vāyic cuttamāka vittirukkat tavaṟutal. vāyp puṇṇil mikavum mukkiyamāṉavi iraṇṭu : 1. tiraṣ vāykkiranti (Thrush), 2. nōmā (Noma) tiraṣ, pāl kuṭikkum paruvattilum, iraṇṭu vayatiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kuḻantikaḷ atika nāṭkaḷ kāyccalāka paṭuttiruntālum, allatu uṭal nalam kuṉṟi nalintiruntālum varum.

nōyk kuṟi : vāyiṉ uṭpuṟattil nākkiṉ mītum, maṟṟiya pākattilum tirintu pōṉa pāl ciṟu kaṭṭikaḷākac citaṟik kiṭappatu pōṉṟu muttip pōṉṟa veṇṇiṟattil paravikkiṭakkum, eḷitil tuṭittu appuṟappaṭutta iyalātu. aḻutti viralāl tuṭittu appuṟappaṭuttiṉāl ciṟiya vaṭṭamāka civanta puṇkaḷik kāṇalām. innōyiṉāl nākkil vīkkamō, valiyō kāṇappaṭuvatilli.



﻿cikicci : taṭṭammi, ṭipāyṭu pōṉṟa nōykaḷil kuḻanti avatippaṭum poḻutu, kuḻantiyiṉ vāyi nāḷtōṟum maruntu nīrāl kaḻuvic cuttamāka vittirukka vēṇṭum. nalivuṟṟa kuḻankiḷukku, ūṭṭamāṉa uṇavu taravēṇṭum. pāl koṭukku muṉ tāyār mārpuk kāmpukaḷic cuttam ceyya vēṇṭum. ceyaṟki uṇavu koṭuppatāṉāl pāl puṭṭi, cuvikkum rappar ivikaḷi ovvoru taṭaviyum payaṉpaṭuttiyapiṉ nīril kotikka vikka vēṇṭum. vāyil ēṟpaṭṭirukkum puṇṇik cuttamāṉa pañciṉāl mellat tuṭittuviṭṭu, nāḷtōṟum, iruvēḷi oru tēkkaraṇṭi aḷavu pōrik amilattuāḷ oru paṭi vennīril karittu, an nīrāl kuḻantiyiṉ vāyic cuttappaṭuttuviṭṭu, nīril karitta jeṉṣiyaṉ vayaleṭ (Gentian violet) eṉṉum maruntip puṇṇiṉ mītu taṭava vēṇṭum. kiḷicariṉ eṉum tiravattuṭaṉ veṇkārattukaḷik (Borax) kalantu, puṇmītu pūcuvatum oruvita cikicciyākum.

nōmā : (Noma) itu vāyppuṇṇil mikavum apāyakaramāṉa nōy, ṭipāyṭu, taṭṭammi pōṉṟa nōykaḷiṉ poḻutu, kuḻantiyiṉ vāy acuttamākak kavaṉikkappaṭāmal puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭāl naccuk kirumikaḷ aṅku ceṉṟu, kuṭiyēṟi, viruttiyaṭintu, vāyil puṇṇi uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa. nalivaṭintirukkum uṭpuṟat tōlip pātittu virivākap paravum ippuṇ cila camayam kaṉṉaṅkaḷil tuvāram uṇṭākki viṭuvatum uṇṭu.

nōykkuṟi : vayiṟṟiṉ mītum, kaṉṉaṅkaḷiṉ ōrattilum mutalil ciṟu kuḻippuṇṇākat toṭaṅkic cila nāṭkaḷil peritākik, karumpacci niṟamāṉa puṇṇākak kāṇappaṭum: turnāṟṟam vīcum. itik kavaṉittuc cikicci ceyyāviṭil maraṇam uṇṭākum.



﻿cikicci : nōyuṟṟa kuḻantikaḷiṉ vāyip poṭṭāciyam permāṅkaṉēṭṭup pōṉṟa maruntu nīril kaḻuvic cuttamāka vittirukka vēṇṭum. peṉiciliṉ, calpāṉilamiṭu pōṉṟavikaḷip puṇ mītu taṭavic cikicci ceyvatuṭaṉ calpāṉilamiṭu uḷḷukkum koṭuppatu nalam. peṉiciliṉ ūciyiṉ mūlam itik kuṇappaṭuttuvatu eḷitu.

nāvil veṭippukkaḷ : nāviṉ mītu kuṟukkum neṭukkumākap piḷavukaḷ allatu veṭippukkaḷ kāṇappaṭuvatuṇṭu. ṭipāyṭu, mēka nōy, ceṅkāyccal (scare fever) pōṉṟa nōykaḷiṉ poḻutu nākkil ciṟu ciṟu piḷavukaḷ kāṇappaṭum.

cikicci : potuvāka, enta vitamāṉa nōyiṉ poḻutum, vāyic cuttamāka vittiruntāl, vāyil puṇṇō, veṭippō ēṟpaṭātu taṭukkalām. veṭippukkaḷ mītu kiḷicariṉ pōrāks eṉṉum tiravattit taṭaviṉāl, ciṟitu kuṇam kāṇappaṭum. mēka nōyiṉāl ēṟpaṭum veṭippukkaḷ, mēka nōykkuc cikikci ceytāl tāṉ maṟiyum.

nōyiṉ pōtu nāviṉ tōṟṟam : uṇavil eñciya kaḻivup poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ, tēkattip pātikkum naccuk kirumikaḷ kalantu koṇṭu, nāviṉ mītu veṇmiyāka māvu paṭintu paraviyatip pōṉṟa oru tōṟṟatti uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa. acīraṇattiṉ poḻutum, kuṭal mantamāka uḷḷapoḻutum nā veṇmiyāṉa māvu pūciyatu pōl, caṟṟu īramākavum kāṇappaṭum. vānti, toṟṟu nōykaḷ, ivikaḷāl kuḻanti avatippaṭum poḻutu, umiḻnīr vaṟṟip pōvatiṉāl nā ularntu kāyntu, veṇṇiṟa māvu pūciyatoru tōṟṟattil kāṇappaṭum. ceṅkāyccal nōyiṉ pōtu, nāviṉ mītuḷḷa melliya tōl nōyuṟṟu, nalintu, nākku raṇamāka, irattac civappākak kāṇappaṭum. itit tavira, uṇavil rīpōpiḷēviṉ eṉappaṭum 'pi' viṭmiṉ pōtāviṭil, nāvil palaviṭaṅkaḷil ciṟu puṇkaḷ kāṇappaṭalām.

paciyiṉmi : kuḻantikaḷukku eḷitāka ēṟpaṭum nōykaḷil itu oṉṟu. 1. caḷippuppōṉṟa cātāraṇat tontaravu mutal, ellā vitamāṉa kāyccalkaḷum. 2. uṇavil tēviyāṉa cattup poruḷkaḷ illāmiyāl uṭampip pīṭikkum nalivu. 3. pal muḷikkum paruvam. 4. ammi kuttuvatāl ēṟpaṭum kāyccal. 5. ceyaṟki ūṭṭattil, oru kuṟippiṭṭa uṇaviṉ mītu calipput taṭṭi viṭutal. 6. kaṇinōy. 7. kuṭaṟpūccikaḷ. 8. kaḷippu. 9. piṭivāta aḻuki.

cikicci : kāraṇam aṟintu cikicci ceytāl paci ēṟpaṭṭu viṭum. uṭalnalattip puṟakkaṇikkātu, ūṭṭamāṉa uṇavukaḷi oḻuṅku muṟi tavaṟātu aḷittāl, paciyiṉmi ēṟpaṭātu. atikap paṭippiṉāl kaḷittup pōkum kuḻantikkut takka ōyvu koṭuttāl paci ēṟpaṭum.

vānti : piṟantavuṭaṉ kuḻanti vānti eṭuppatiṉ kāraṇam, piṟanta kuḻantikku varum nōykaḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. maṟṟavi iṅkē kāṇalām.

vāntiyiṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷ : 1. uṇavu aḷikkappaṭum muṟikaḷil ēṟpaṭum tavaṟukaḷ: kuḻanti virivil vaḷaravēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvattil, tēvikku mīṟiya aḷavu uṇavu aḷippatu, aḷikkum aḷavil oḻuṅkumuṟi illāmi, ottuk koḷḷāta uṇavukaḷi aḷittal, uṇavuṭaṉ kuḻanti kāṟṟiyum viḻuṅkutal. 2. ﻿palavakik kāyccalkaḷ, toṟṟu nōykaḷ. 3. acīraṇam. 4. cila kuḻantikaḷukkup piṟaviyilēyē kuṭaliṉ vāy mikavum kuṟukalāka amintuviṭuvatuṇṭu. 5. kuṭal ēṟṟam, kuṭal vāl aḻaṟci (Instussusception and appendicts) 6. mūḷiyil kaṭṭikaḷ. 7. uḷnākku nīṇṭu vaḷarvatu. itaṉāl irumal ēṟpaṭṭu, vānti ēṟpaṭalām. 8. avacaramāka uṇavi viḻuṅkutal. 9. kuḻantikaḷ payattālō, kōpattālō maṉam kuḻampiyirukkum nili. 10. vaṇṭi, kappal, vimāṉam ivikaḷil pirayāṇam ceyvatiṉāl ēṟpaṭum oruvita mayakkam. 11. kāramāṉa naccup poruḷi viḻuṅkiviṭuvatu mutaliyaṉa. 12. maṉattukkup piṭikkāta uṇavi vaṟpuṟutti uṭkoḷḷac ceytal. 13. piṟavik kōḷāṟāka aminta kuṟukiya irippi vāy.

atika vānti : itit tirumpat tirumpa varum naccu vānti eṉṟum aḻippatuṇṭu. (Cyclic vomiting or periodic vomiting). iti oru taṉippaṭṭa nōy eṉpativiṭap palvēṟu nōykaḷiṉ aṟikuṟi eṉṟu kūṟuvatu poruttamākum. inta nōy oru vayatiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kuḻantikaḷiyē peritum pātikkiṉṟatu. payaṅkoḷḷik kuḻantikaḷ, uṇarcci vacappaṭṭa kuḻantikaḷ ākiyavarkaḷē, itaṟku atikam ilakkākiṉṟaṉar. mātattiṟku orumuṟi allatu irumuṟi, āṇṭiṟku mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku taṭavikaḷ innōy kuḻantiyit tirumpat tirumpa vantu pātikkalām. oru taṭavi vanta vānti nillāmal toṭarntāṟpōl, nāṭkaṇakkil vantu uṭal nalatti mikavum keṭukkakkūṭum. inta vānti nōy vantirukkum camayam, ciṟunīril kīṭṭōṉ eṉṉum naccupporuḷ veḷiyēṟik koṇṭirukkum. ākavē iti naccu vānti eṉa aḻippatu mikka poruttamākum. kāraṇam iṉṉum viḷakkamuṟavilli.



﻿nōykkuṟikaḷ: vayiṟṟuvali, malakcikkal, kumaṭṭal, uṭaṟcōrvu, acati, paciyiṉmi ivi pōṉṟa kuṟikaḷuṭaṉ, vānti toṭarntu ēṟpaṭalām; allatu eṭutta eṭuppilēyē vānti ārampamākalām. mutal vāntiyil cīraṇamākāta uṇavu veḷippaṭum. toṭarntu ēṟpaṭum vāntikaḷil, kōḻiyum, pitta nīrum kalantu kāṇappaṭum. piṉṉar veḷiyākum vāntikaḷil, kōḻi, pittanīr ivikaḷuṭaṉ irattamum ciṟitu kalantu kāṇappaṭum. mikavum tīviramāṉa camayaṅkaḷil arimaṇikkoru taṭavi vānti nillāmal vantu koṇṭē irukkum. tākam, talivali, vayiṟṟu nōy mutaliyavi mukkiyamāṉa aṟikuṟikaḷ, nāvāṉatu kāyntu vaṟaṭciyākak kāṇappaṭum. mūccil oruvita puḻukka vācaṉi vīcum. tōl vaṟaṇṭu, ciṟitu cūṭākak kāṇappaṭum. kaṇkaḷ kuḻi viḻuntu, viḻikaḷil ēkkappārvi kāṇappaṭum.

cikicci : kuḻantiyi naṭamāṭa viṭātu, paṭukkiyil ōyvākak kiṭatta vēṇṭum. uṇavu koṭukkak kūṭātu. ularum vāyil īram uṇṭākkac ciṟitaḷavu ais appōtikkap pōtu koṭukkalām. nōy ati tīvaramāka illāviṭil, kuḷakkōcu kalanta, āṟiya vennīr allatu cukkup pōṭṭuk kotikka vittu āṟiya vennīr, tēkkaraṇṭi aḷavu arimaṇikkoru taṭavi koṭukkalām.

itittavira, uppunīril kalanta 5 % kuḷukkōcu tiravatti, ūci mūlam cumār 200-400 ka.ce. koṭukka vēṇṭum, kōṭīṉ, cōṭiyam piṉōpārpiṭāl pōṉṟa maruntukaḷik koṭuttu vāntiyi niṟutta vēṇṭum. ﻿kakkal : cila camayam kuḻantikaḷ cāppiṭṭa uṇavi uṭaṉukkuṭaṉēyē kakki viṭuvatuṇṭu. itu, oru vayatukkuṭpaṭṭa kuḻantiyiṭamē atikam kāṇappaṭum.

cikicci : uṇavu uṇṭavuṭaṉ vāyi aḻuttamāka mūṭic ciṟitu nēram piṭittiruntāl, kuḻanti uṇavik kakkuvatilli. uṇavu koṭukkumpōtu, kuḻantiyiṉ kavaṉatti viḷiyāṭṭil tiruppi viṭuvatum, itit taṭukkum muṟikaḷil oṉṟu.

pēti kuḻantip paruvattil tontaravaḷikkum nōykaḷil oṉṟu. itu mutal vayatil aṭikkaṭi varuvatu. itu 1. cātāraṇa pēti. 2. naccuk kirumikaḷālāṉa pēti. 3. piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷāl ēṟpaṭum pēti, eṉṟu mūvakippaṭum.

mutalvakiyiṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷ : 1. uṇavūṭṭattāl ēṟpaṭum tavaṟu: uṇavil koḻuppup poruḷ mikuntiruntāl, veḷutta vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa pētiyum, purōṭṭīṉ mikuntiruntāl, tayirkkaṭṭi pōṉṟa pētiyum, sṭārccu atikamiruntāl kaṭṭiyum nuriyumāṉa pētiyum uṇṭākum. 2. cōrvu, kaḷippu, kōpatāpa uṇarccikaḷ. 3. tirāyṭu curappuk kuṟivu. 4. cōki. 5. irattattil amilam mikutal. 6. vēkāta uṇavup poruḷkaḷ, paḻukkāta kaṉikaḷ mutaliya uṇṇutal.

iraṇṭāvatu vaki: mutal vaki pētiyiṉ poḻutu, kuṭaliṉ nalam kuṟintu, nalintu naccukkirumikaḷ tākki, mēlum pētiyi uṇṭākkalām.

piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷāl ēṟpaṭum pēti : kuṭali iyakkuvikkum narampukaḷ, cila kuḻantikaḷukku mutal vayatil nalla uṟutiyāṉa niliyil iruppatilli. ﻿ippaṭippaṭṭa kuḻantikaḷ uṇavu uṭkoṇṭa uṭaṉēyē, oru muṟi nīrāka malam kaḻippatuṇṭu. itit tavira ṭipāyṭu, nimōṉiyā pōṉṟa kāyccaliṉ pōtum kuḻantikkup pēti varuvatu vaḻakkam. cātāraṇa pētiyākat toṭaṅkuvatu, piṟaku cītapētiyāka māṟuvatum uṇṭu.

viṣakkirumikaḷāl ēṟpaṭum pēti : perumpālum kōṭi kālattilē varuvatāl, kōṭi kāla pētiyeṉavum aḻikkappaṭum inta pētikkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ: 1. kirumikaḷik kolla utavum hiṭirō kuḷōrik amilam kuḻantikaḷiṉ irippiyil kuṟivāka iruppatu. 2. pāl allatu vēṟu uṇavup poruḷ naṉṟākak kāyccic cuttappaṭuttātu aḷippatu. 3. nalla veyil allatu nalla maḻi ivi iraṇṭum, kirumikaḷ viruttiyaṭiya ukanta kālam.

inta vaki pēti, kuḻantiyi mikavum kaṭumiākap pātikkiṉṟatu. itil mūṉṟu vakikaḷ uṇṭu. 1. pattup patiṉintu taṭavikaḷ malam maṭṭum veḷippaṭum cātāraṇa vaki. 2. kāyccaluṭaṉ pēti, 3. kālarā nōyip pōl nīrāṉa malam veḷippaṭṭu, apāyam viḷivikkum koṭiya pēti.

nōykkuṟikaḷ : tiṭīreṉṟu aṭikkaṭi iḷakalāṉa malam kaḻikkat tuvaṅkip piṟaku pētiyāka muṭivaṭiyalām. mutal taṭavi keṭṭiyāka veḷippaṭṭa malam, aṭutta muṟi ciṟitu nekiḻntu, muṭivil nīrāka veḷip paṭalām. mutalil mañcaḷ niṟamāka irunta malam, ciṟitu poḻutil pacci niṟatti aṭintu, muṭivil irattamum cītamumākak kāṇappaṭalām. pēti mikavum kaṭumiyāka māṟiviṭṭāl, malam cāṇinīr pōṉṟa tōṟṟattil mikavum turnāṟṟattuṭaṉ irukkum.



﻿pētiyiṉāl, kuḻantiyiṉ uṭal nili mikavum pātikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. uṭalil uḷḷa nīr atikamāka veḷiyēṟi viṭuvataṉāl, uṭalukkut tēviyāṉa taṇṇīr kuṟintuviṭukiṉṟatu. naccukkirumikaḷāl uṇṭākum nañcu ellā uṟuppukkaḷiyum pātippatiṉāl, avi cōrntuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē tōl taḷarntu kāṇappaṭum. nāṭi mikavum melivākat teṉpaṭum. kaṇkaḷ kuḻi viḻuntu, kaṇṇimikaḷ viḻiyi muḻuvatum mūṭac caktiyaṟṟup pāti tiṟanta niliyil irukkum. uṭaliṉ veppa nili kuṟintu kāṇappaṭum. utaṭukaḷ kaṟuttuviṭuvatu pēti mikavum kaṭumiyākiviṭṭataṉ kuṟiyākum.

cikikci : kampaḷiyāl uṭali naṉṟākap pōrtti, uṭalveppam kuṟiyātu kākkavēṇṭum. mikavum kaṭumiyāṉa pētikku maruttuva cāliyil vittuc cikicci ceyvatu tāṉ takunta muṟi. uṭaliliruntu pirinta nīric carikkaṭṭa, piḷāsmā, 5% kuḷukkōcut tiravam, ēṟkeṉavē cōkiyiṉāl pātikkappaṭṭa kuḻantiyāka iruntāl, irattam mutaliyavaṟṟic coṭṭu coṭṭāka ūcimūlam ēṟṟa vēṇṭiyirukkum. uṇavu ciṟitaḷavu ēṟkum niliyil iruntāl pāl pōṉṟa uṇavukaḷi niṟuttiviṭṭup pārli nīr, kuḷukkōcu nīr ivikaḷi maṭṭumē koñca aḷavil ari maṇikkoru taṭavi koṭukka vēṇṭum. kuṭal vēkamāka iyaṅkuvati niṟutta, kēyōliṉ, pismattu mutaliyavi uḷḷukkuk koṭukkavēṇṭum. pēti atikamāki valippu ēṟpaṭa iruntāl, kālciyam kuḷukkōṉēṭṭu marunti ūci mūlam ēṟṟavēṇṭi varum. itit tavira, viṭṭamiṉ ci, pi akiyavaṟṟi vēṇṭiya aḷavu ūci mūlam ēṟṟavēṇṭum. naccuk kirumikaḷik kollavalla calpāṉilamiṭu maruntukaḷiyum koṭukka vēṇṭum.



﻿vayiṟṟuvali : kāraṇam 1. pālil uṇṭākum lākṭik amilam kuṭaliṉ uṭpuṟattuḷḷa melliya toli uṟuttuvatu. 2. cīraṇamākāta purōṭṭīṉ peruṅkaṭṭikaḷākak kuṭalil tēṅkik kiṭappatu. 3. cīraṇak kōḷāṟṟiṉāl kuṭalil vāyuttēkkam ēṟpaṭuvatu.

nōyk kuṟikaḷ: 1. kuḻanti tiṭīr eṉṟu aḻum. ciṟitu nēram aḻutu tuṭitta piṉpu tāṉē ōyum. maṟupaṭiyum tiṭīreṉṟu vīṟiṭṭu aḻum. paci, kātuvali, ciṟunīrakattil kal, kaṇi nōy, mūḷi mantam mutaliyaṉa ēṟpaṭukiyilum, kuḻanti mēṟcoṉṉavāṟu viṭṭuviṭṭu aḻakkūṭum. 2. vayiṟu kalpōl kaṭiṉamākak kikkut teṉpaṭum. 3. aḻum poḻutu kuḻanti kālkaḷiraṇṭiyum mēlukkut tūkkik koṇṭu aḻum. 4. atikamākat tuṭittu aḻutāl, iḻuppu ēṟpaṭalām. 5. vayiṟṟil tēṅkiyirukkum kāṟṟu veḷippaṭṭavuṭaṉ caṭṭeṉa aḻuki oyntu pōkum.

cikicci : vayiṟṟiṉ mītu lēcākac ciṟitaḷavu ṭarppaṉṭṭiṉ eṇṇiyip pūci, vennīrp piyiṉāl ottaṭam koṭukka vēṇṭum. vayiṟṟiṉ mītu āḷiviti allatu kaṭuku arittup pūcuvatum nalam. malattēkkam illāmal ceyyak kiḷicariṉum, vennīrum kalanta eṉimā koṭuttuk kuṭalic cuttappaṭuttal nalam. kuṭalil tēṅkiyirukkum vāyuvi veḷippaṭutta, iraṇṭu allatu nāṉku coṭṭup pepparmiṉṭu nīr koṭuppatu nalam. āṉāl aṭikkaṭi vayiṟṟu vali kuḻantikku varātu taṭuppatu tāṉ ukanta cikicciyākum. tāyppāl maṭṭum uṇṇum kuḻantikaḷukkup pāl koṭukkum vēḷikaḷi oḻuṅkākak kavaṉittuk koṭukka vēṇṭum. niṉitta poḻutellām tāyppāl aḷittal tavaṟu. pāl eḷitil cīraṇamaṭivataṟkāka iraṇṭu kireyiṉ ﻿aḷavu cēṭiyam ciṭrēṭṭu uppit taṇṇīril karittup pukaṭṭi viṭuvatu nalam. malakcikkal illāmal kuḻantiyik kavaṉittuk koḷvatu, cikicci muṟikaḷil oṉṟākum. ivvaḷavu kavaṉittum tāypāl kuṭikkum kuḻantikku aṭikkaṭi vayiṟṟu vali ēṟpaṭṭāl, tāyp pāli niṟuttiviṭṭu, vēṟu uṇavik koṭuppatu nalam.

ceyaṟki uṇavu tantāl uṇavi mikavum cuttamākat tayārikka vēṇṭum. cōṭā pik kārpaṉēṭṭu uppu 2% kireyiṉ, aromāṭṭik spiriṭ āp ammōṉiya 2% coṭṭu, kiḷicariṉ 2% coṭṭu, itaṉuṭaṉ oru tēkkaraṇṭi aḷavu pepparmiṉṭu nīr kalantu, uṇavu tarum vēḷikaḷukkiṭiyē cila nāṭkaḷ koṭuttu vantāl, kuḻantikku varum vayiṟṟu valiyi oruvāṟu taṭukka iyalum. oru kuṟippiṭṭa ceyaṟki uṇavu ottukkoḷḷāmal vayiṟṟuvali ēṟpaṭṭāl, ati māṟṟi vēṟu vaki uṇavit taravēṇṭum.

malaccikkal kuḻantikaḷukku varum cātāraṇa nōykaḷil oṉṟu. itippaṟṟi vēṟiṭattil vivarikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

vikkal : kuḻanti piṟanta mutal cila mātaṅkaḷil, uṇaviṟkup piṟaku vikkal varuvatu iyaṟki. itaṉāl apāyam illi. āṉāl, avacaramākap pālip parukum poḻutu itaṉuṭaṉ kāṟṟiyum viḻuṅki viṭuvatiṉāl apāyam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. ataṉāl avacarappaṭāmal, nitāṉittu aḷikka vēṇṭum. pāl kuṭittatum, kuḻantiyit tōḷiṉ mītu cārttik koṇṭu, mella mutukil nālintu muṟi taṭṭikkoṭuttāl, viḻuṅkiyuḷḷa kāṟṟu veḷiyēṟiviṭum. koñcam vetu vetuppāṉa vennīr, kuṭippataṟku aḷittāl, cila camayam vikkal niṉṟu pōvatuṇṭu.



cīraṇak karuvikaḷip paṟṟiya piṟa nōykaḷ : 1. piṟavilēyē kuṭal vāy kuṟukiyiruttal. 2. kuṭal taṭi, kuṭal vāl aḻaṟci. 3. īral kulikkaṭṭi, 4. kallīral, maṇṇīral vīkkam. 5. kuṭal iṟakkam. 6. mañcaḷ kāmāli mutaliyaṉa. ivaṟṟippaṟṟi vēṟu iṭattil vivaram uḷḷatu.

iḷittal : kuḻanti iḷittup pōvatiṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷ piṉvaruvaṉa : 1. ūṭṭakkuṟi (a) kuḻantikku aḷikkappaṭum uṇavu vaḷarccikku vēṇṭiya aḷavillāmal kuṟivāka iruppatu (b) uṇavi vēṇṭiya aḷavu uṭkoḷḷa muṭiyāmal, mūḷi utaṭu, mūḷi mēlvāy pōṉṟa piṟavik kōḷāṟukaḷ iruppatu. (c) tēviyāṉa cattupporuḷkaḷ kuṟinta, ceyaṟki uṇavi aḷippatu. 2. kuḻanti kuṟi mātattil piṟappatu. itaṟkuc cīraṇa cakti kuṟivu. tēviyāṉa pālik kuṭikka uṭalil cakti iruppatilli. uṇavil perumpakuti uṭaliṉ veppa niliyik kākkac celavaḻintu pōvatiṉāl vaḷarccikkut tēviyāṉa aḷavu uṇavu kiṭippatilli. 3. kuḻantikku aṭikkaṭi varum pēti nōy, 4. acīraṇam. 5. piṟaviyilēyē ēṟpaṭum mēka nōy. 6. kṣayam. 7. evvitak kāraṇamuṉṟi iḷittup pōkum oru taṉippaṭṭa nōy . (Idiopathic wasting)

cikicci : iḷikkum kāraṇattik kaṇṭupiṭittut taṭuppatutāṉ takunta cikicci. ūṭṭamāṉa uṇavu, kāṟṟōṭṭam, cūriya veḷiccam mutaliyaṉa iḷittatit taṭukkum. iḷittirukkum kuḻantiyik kuḷippāṭṭiyatum, īram pōka naṉṟākat tuṭikka vēṇṭum. nōyuṟṟu nalintuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷi maṭiyilēyē vittuk kuḻantikkuc cāntiyi ūṭṭuvatu mikavum mukkiyamāṉatu. ittakiya kuḻantikaḷukkut tāyiṉ kavaṉippu mikavum atikam tēvi.



acīraṇam : kuḻantip paruvattil, acīraṇam mūṉṟu vakippaṭum. 1. irippi acīraṇam (a) tīviravaki. (b) aṭikkaṭi ēṟpaṭum mitamāṉa oru vaki. 2. īral acīraṇam. 3. kuṭal acīraṇam.

irippi acīraṇam : (a) tīviramāṉatu : itic cātāraṇamāka vayiṟu keṭṭuviṭṭatu. pittam koṇṭuviṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟuvārkaḷ.

kāraṇam : paḻukkāta kāykaḷ, tēṅkāy mutaliyavikaḷi aḷavukku mīṟi uṇpatu.

nōykkuṟi : acīraṇappaṭuttum uṇavu vakikaḷi uṇṭa cila maṇi nērattil, kuḻanti vayiṟṟu nōvil tuṭittukkoṇṭu, vānti eṭukkum. cila camayam kāyccalum cērntu kāṇappaṭum.

cikicci : nōyāḷiyip paṭṭiṉi pōṭṭuviṭuvatu nalla cikicci muṟi. vānti eṭuttavuṭaṉ vayiṟṟu nōvu niṉṟuviṭum. oru kirām aḷavu cōṭā uppi, irupatu avuṉs nīril karittu, anta nīri oru tēkkaraṇṭiyaḷavu pattu nimiṣattukku oru muṟi koṭukkavēṇṭum. eṉimā koṭuttu kuṭalic cuttappaṭuttiviṭṭu, vayiṟṟiṉ mītu vennīrppi vittu ottaṭam koṭuttāl, nōvu kuṟiyum. kuḻanti orumuṟi vānti eṭutta piṉpu, pismattup pōṉṟa maruntukaḷik koṭuppatu nalam.

aṭikkaṭi ēṟpaṭum mitamāṉa acīraṇam : kāraṇaṅkaḷ 1. tīvira acīraṇam kuṇamākāmal, piṟaku aṭikkaṭi tōṉṟit tontaravu koṭukkalām. 2. cilavaki uṇavup poruḷkaḷ, kuṭaliṉ uṭpuṟat tōlukkut tontaravu koṭuppataṉāl inta vaki acīraṇam ēṟpaṭalām. 3 askā-carkkari atikam kalanta tittippup paṇṭaṅkaḷ. 4. uṇavup poruḷkaḷi naṉṟāka meṉṟu cāppiṭātu viḻuṅkutal. 5. avacaramākac cuṭaccuṭa uṇavic cāppiṭuvatu. 6. atikak koḻuppuc cattu mikunta miṭṭāykaḷ.

nōyk kuṟikaḷ : paciyiṉmi, kāli vēḷikaḷil taliccuṟṟal, kumaṭṭal, kuṭalil kāṟṟu niṟintu tuṉpuṟuttal, cāppiṭṭavuṭaṉ vayiṟṟuvali, aṭikkaṭi mūrcci, aṭuppukkari, maṇ pōṉṟavikaḷ tiṉṉum keṭṭa paḻakkam.

cikicci : cīraṇatti mantappaṭuttum poruḷkaḷi, uṇaviṉiṉṟum nīkkiviṭa vēṇṭum. aṭikkaṭi acīraṇam ēṟpaṭṭāl, oru muṟi pētikkuk koṭuppatu mikavum nallatu.

īral acīraṇam : mūṉṟu vayatukkuṭpaṭṭa kuḻantikaḷiṭamē atikam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.

kāraṇam : 1. parampariyākac cila kuṭumpattil uḷḷavarkaḷukku kallīral mantamāka vēli ceyyum taṉmi kāraṇamākalām. 2. pāl, veṇṇey pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷi atikamāka uṇavil cērppatāl, kallīral atikamāka vēli ceytu cōrntu pōkalām.

nōykkuṟi : paciyiṉmi, kuḻantiyiṉ uṭal eṭi kuṟintu koṇṭu varuvatu, tūkkamiṉmi, viḷiyāṭṭil cōrvu, kaṇ ericcal, aḻuki, piṭivātam mutaliyaṉa. ivaṟṟuṭaṉ kuḻantiyiṉ mūccil turnāṟṟam vīcuvatiyum, malam mañcaḷ niṟamāka illātu veḷḷiyāka iruppatiyum kāṇalām. maṟṟum, cila camayam talivali, vānti, kāyccal mutaliyaṉavum kāṇappaṭalām.

cikicci : cīraṇattip pātikkum uṇavu vakikaḷi nīkki, eḷitil cīraṇikkum uṇavu vakikaḷic cērkka vēṇṭum. oḻuṅkāṉa nērattil cāppiṭum paḻakkam ēṟpaṭutta vēṇṭum. mēlum kire poṭi (Grey powder) 12 kireyiṉ allatu rūpārp (Rhubarb) 1/2 kireyiṉ eṉṟa aḷavil paṭukkumpōtu, oruvāra kālam koṭukkalām.

kuṭal acīraṇam: aintiliruntu eṭṭu vayatiṟkuṭpaṭṭa kuḻantikaḷiṭam atikam kāṇappaṭum.

kāraṇam : 1. irippi, kuṭal ivikaḷil atikappaṭiyākak kōḻi uṇṭāki, cīraṇa nīr kalantu ataṉuṭiya urattik kulittu viṭutal. 2. kuṭalil kaciyum kōḻi, aṅkē taṅkiyuḷḷa malattiṉ mītu paṭintu viṭuvatiṉāl, kuṭal malatti veḷiyē taḷḷa muṭiyāmalāki malaccikkal ēṟpaṭutal. malaccikkalāl ēṟpaṭum nañcu irattatil kalantu acīraṇam uṇṭu paṇṇukiṟatu. 3. uṇavil atika carkkariyum purōṭṭīṉum iruttal, atika vēli, toṇṭiyil cati vaḷarntu puṇṇākutal.

cikicci : kuḻantiyiṉ uṇavil oḻuṅkumuṟi vēṇṭum. kuḻantikkut takunta ōyvum, vēṟu poḻutu pōkkukaḷum vēṇṭum. cīraṇattik keṭukkum cāklēṭṭup pōṉṟa koḻuppuk paṇṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟi nīkka vēṇṭum. orumuṟi pētikkuk koṭuppatu nalam. paciyit tūṇṭum kacappu maruntukaḷ (Bitters) cilavaṟṟik koṭuppatu nalam. iravu vēḷikaḷil, oru tēkkaraṇṭi viḷakkeṇṇey, mali vāḻippaḻam, allatu or avuṉs pārapiṉ eṇṇey pōṉṟavikaḷik koṭuttu, malaccikkaliṉṟik kāppatu nalam.

caḷippu : mūkkiṉ uṭpuṟamuḷḷa melliya tōlip pātittu, atit toṭarntuḷḷa tuḷikaḷiyum pātikkum nōy, caḷippu eṉṟum, nīrkkōvi eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.

kāraṇam : inta nōykkuk kāraṇam, oru vakiyāṉa viras kirumiyākum. ivi oruvariṭamiruntu iṉṉoruvarukku nōyip parappum āṟṟal koṇṭavi. nōyiṉ toṭakkattiṟku viras kirumikaḷ kāraṇam eṉiṉum, ataṟkup piṟaku, mūkkil caḷi kacintu keṭṭiyāka veḷippaṭuvataṟku, vēṟu caḷi naccuk kirumikaḷ kāraṇamākiṉṟaṉa. inta kirumikaḷ vantu kuṭiyēṟi, viruttiyaṭintu, caḷi nōyi uṟpatti ceyya cila taṉippaṭṭa kāraṇaṅkaḷ mukkiyamāka uṇṭu. 1 vayatu piṟantatiliruntu cumār aintu mātam vari caḷippu, kuḻantikaḷukku atikam varuvatilli. intap paruvattil mūkkiṉ uṭpuṟattōl, viras kirumikaḷ viruttiyaṭiya ēṟṟatāka iruppatilli. ituvē kāraṇam eṉpatu oru cārār koḷki. aintu mātaṅkaḷukku mēṟpaṭṭa kuḻanti aṭikkaṭi caḷi piṭittuk koḷvatu iyaṟki. 2. ūṭṭak kuṟivu, viṭṭamiṉkaḷ, ciṟappāka viṭṭamiṉ ē kuṟivāka iruttal. 3. toṇṭic cati, aṭiṉāyṭu, mūkkaṭittuvāraṅkaḷ (sinuses) mutaliyavikaḷil toṟṟu ēṟpaṭutal. 4. tūciyuḷḷa iṭattilum, kuṟinta kāṟṟōṭṭamuḷḷa iṭattilum vacittal. 5. atikak kuḷiril iruttal, maḻiyil naṉital, kūṭṭamāṉa iṭattil atika nēram iruntu viṭṭu, caṭṭeṉak kāṟṟōṭṭamāṉa tiṟanta veḷikku varuvatu. 6. tali muḻukiya piṉpu taliyi naṉṟākat tuvaṭṭāmal īramāka viṭṭuvittal. 7. paci, kuḻantiyiṉ uṭal nalattik keṭuttuc caḷik kirumikaḷ kuṭiyēṟi viruttiyaṭiya aṉukūlam ceytal.

nōyk kuṟikaḷ : oru vayatiṟkuṭpaṭṭa kuḻanti, aṭikkaṭi aḻum. mukam civantu kāṇappaṭum. uṭal ilēcāṉa cūṭākat teṉpaṭum. kaṇkaḷilum mūkkilum nīr kacintu koṇṭirukkum. paci keṭṭu viṭum. periyavarkaḷip pōl mūkkil kaciyum nīri appuṟappaṭutta muṭiyātatiṉāl, tēṅkiyuḷḷa caḷi nīr mūccit tiṇaṟac ceyyum. mēlum, kuḻantiyiṉ mūkkut tuvāram, periyavarkaḷukku iruppativiṭac ciṟiyatākiyiṉāl, uḷḷēyuḷḷa tōl uppik koṇṭu, tuvāram kuṟintu mūccut tiṇaṟal ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.

oru vayatiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kuḻanti, kāliyil naṉṟāka viḷiyāṭik koṇṭirukkum. naṭuppakaliṉ pōtu cōrntu kāṇappaṭum. cāyaṅkālam mūkkip kikaḷāl tēyttuk koṇṭu, piṭivātamum aḻukiyumāka māṟiviṭum. kāyccal 102 : pā. allatu 103 pā. vari kāṇappaṭum. kaṇkaḷ civantu, imikaḷ iṭuṅkum. mūkkilum, kaṇkaḷilum nīr curantu, mūccut tiṇaṟum. cila camayam kuḻantikkuk caḷi piṭippatu vāntiyuṭaṉ ārampamāvatum uṇṭu. kāyccalillāmal veṟum caḷimaṭṭumē kāṇalām.

caḷippiṉāl ēṟpaṭum tontaravukaḷ : caḷippu apāyaramāṉa nōyāka illāviṭiṉum, atit toṭarntu vēṟu nōykaḷ toṭaṅkak kūṭum. mūkkiṉ uṭpuṟat tōlip pātikkum naccukkirumikaḷ kātu, toṇṭi, cuvācak kuḻalkaḷ, mūkkaṭit tuvāraṅkaḷ, kaḻuttuc curappikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum paravip palvēṟu nōykaḷi uṇṭu paṇṇalām. kātil cīḻ vaṭital, toṇṭi raṇam, mūḷic caṉṉi, nimōṉiyā, irumal pōṉṟa nōykaḷ caḷi kāraṇamāka uṇṭākalām.

cikicci : kuḻantikkuc caḷi ēṟpaṭātu taṭuppatu tāṉ takunta cikicciyākum. kūṭṭam niṟinta nāṭaka, ciṉimāk koṭṭakikaḷukkuḷ kuḻantiyi eṭuttuppōtal kūṭātu. caḷi piṭittiruppavar kuḻantiyi eṭuttuk koñci viḷiyāṭi muttamiṭutal ākātu.

kuḷirkālattil kampaḷic caṭṭikaḷip pōṭṭu atikak kuḷir kāṟṟu mēlē vīcātu taṭuttal vēṇṭum. viṭṭamiṉ ‘ē’ niṟinta uṇavu, tēviyāṉa ōyvu, cūriya veḷiccam, nalla kāṟṟu, ivikaḷ caḷi varāmal pātukākkum maruntukaḷ. nōy vāyppaṭṭirukkum kuḻantiyi, ōyvākap paṭukkiyil kiṭatti vippatu nalam. oru vayatiṟku oru kireyiṉ aḷavu eṉṟa kaṇakkil oru taṭavikkuc cumār aintu kireyiṉukku mēṟpaṭāta āspariṉ (ta.ka) maruntu uḷḷukkuk koṭuppatu oruvita cikikciyākum. āṉālum, āspariṉ marunti niṉitta māttiram, tāṟu māṟākap payaṉpaṭuttuvatu tavaṟu. tūṅkuvataṟkuḷ kōṭīṉ allatu pārpiṭrēṭṭu ētēṉum koṭukkalām. kuḻantikku eṉimā koṭuttuk kuṭalic cuttappaṭuttuvatu nalam. caḷi cuvācak kuḻalukkuḷ pōy viṭātirukkap paṭukkiyil kālkaḷi uyartti, taliyit tāḻtti vikka vēṇṭum; kuḻantiyik kuppuṟap paṭukkavittut tūṅkac ceyvatu nalla muṟiyākum. caḷi nīri melliya pañcut tuṇiyiṉāl appuṟappaṭutta vēṇṭum. oru vayatiṟkuṭpaṭṭa kuḻantikkup pārlit taṇṇīrum, kuḷukkōcu carkkariyum kalanta eḷiya uṇavu pōtum. ataṟku mēṟpaṭṭa vayatuṭiya kuḻantikaḷukkuk keṭṭiyāṉa uṇavi nīkkiviṭṭup pāl, paḻaccāṟu mutaliya nīrtta uṇavu aḷippatu tāṉ nallatu. caḷi nōykku ikkāla vittiyarkaḷ, calpāṉamiṭu, peṉiciliṉ pōṉṟa maruntukaḷip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, im maruntukaḷ caḷiyip pōkkum maruntukaḷ eṉa niccayamākak kūṟa muṭiyātu.



irumal : itu oru taṉinōy alla ; uṭalip pātikkum nōykaḷiṉ aṟikuṟi. itil iraṇṭu vakikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. taṉiyē varum irumal. 2. kāyccaluṭaṉ kāṇappaṭum irumal. irumal kuḻantikaḷukku aṭikkaṭi varum nōykaḷil oṉṟu.

irumalukku kāraṇam : 1. nuriyīralkaḷukkuc cellum ciṟu kuḻalkaḷil iraṇam eṟpaṭṭu, atiṉiṉṟu kaciyum caḷi, kuḻalukkuḷḷē taṅki mūccu muṭṭali uṇṭu paṇṇukiyil, uṭal ati nivarttikkac caḷiyi appuṟappaṭuttac ceyyum muyaṟci irumal. inta nōy kāyccaluṭaṉ varalām; taṉiyākavum varalām. 2. toṇṭiyilum, mūkkilum, cati vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭṭiruttal. 3. uḷnākku nīṇṭu vaḷarntu iruppatu. 4. paruvakāla vēṟṟumiyiṉāl toṇṭiyil iraṇam ēṟpaṭuvatu.

nōyk kuṟikaḷ : toṇṭic cati, aṭiṉāyṭu ivikaḷiṉ vīkkattiṉāl irumal ēṟpaṭṭāl, kuḻanti mūccut tiṇaṟak, kaṇkaḷil nīr peruki irumum. iraviltāṉ irumal atikam kāṇappaṭum. irumal niṉṟatum, cāppiṭṭa uṇavu vānti eṭukkum. toṇṭiyil iraṇam ēṟpaṭṭatāl kāṇum irumal, caḷiyaṟṟa vaṟaṭciyāṉa irumalāka irukkum. toṇṭiyil īram ularntavuṭaṉ aṭikkaṭi ēṟpaṭum.

uḷnākku nīṇṭatāl ēṟpaṭum irumal ; kuḻanti paṭuttukkoḷḷum poḻutu, uḷnākku vāyiṉ piṉ pākattiṉ mītu paṭivatāl, uṟutti irumal ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. itu caḷiyaṟṟa, vaṟaṭci irumalāka irukkum. iravu vēḷikaḷil tāṉ atikam kāṇappaṭum.

mūccuk kuḻalaṟciyāl ēṟpaṭum irumal; cila camayam irumaluṭaṉ kāyccalum cērntu kāṇappaṭum. irumaluṭaṉ kōḻi veḷippaṭum. irumi veḷiyēṟum kōḻiyi veḷiyē tuppat teriyāta kuḻanti, ati viḻuṅki viṭuvataṉāl malattilum vāntiyilum kōḻiyik kāṇalām. itit tavira toṇṭi, mārpu, mutuku mutaliya iṭattil kiyi vittup pārttāl, karkar eṉṟa oliyi uṇaralām. kōḻi niṟintuḷḷa mūccuk kuḻalkaḷukkuḷ cuvācam ciramappaṭṭu veḷiyēṟum poḻutu, ivvitamāṉa oli uṇṭākiṟatu.

cikicci : irumaliṉ kāraṇattip poṟuttuḷḷatu. toṇṭi kāyntu pōvatiṉāl, irumal varuvatāka iruntāl, paṭukkap pōkum muṉ kuḻantikku nalla cūṭāṉa pāl koñcam koṭukka vēṇṭum. māṇṭil speyiṇṭu, ricārlāṉ peyṉṭu pōṉṟa toṇṭikkut taṭavum maruntukaḷit taṭaviṉālum irumal kuṟiyum. kuḻantiyiṉ uṇavil viṭṭamiṉ ‘ē’ atikam iruntāl in nōy varāmal irukkātu. inta irumalil avatippaṭum kuḻantikkut tiṉacari iraṇṭu tēkkaraṇṭiyaḷavu mīṉ eṇṇey paṭukkappōkum muṉ koṭuppatu nalam. uḷnākku nīṇṭiruppatāl ēṟpaṭum irumalukku nīṇṭa pākattik kattarittuc carippaṭutti viṭṭāl irumal niṉṟuviṭum. toṇṭic catiyum, aṭiṉāyṭum vīṅkiyatāl ēṟpaṭum irumalukkut toṇṭi, mūkku, kātu vittiya nipuṇariṭam cicicci peṟuvatu nalam.

kāyccal : itu oru taṉippaṭṭa nōy alla. pala nōykaḷiṉ veḷippaṭiyāṉa oru ciṉṉam. caḷippu nōyiliruntu toṟṟu nōykaḷ vari, enta vitamāṉa nōyum, kāyccal ciṉṉattuṭaṉ tāṉ toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. cila camayam kuḻantikkuk cātāraṇamāka uṭal veppanili, māli vēḷikaḷil 990 pā. allatu 1000 pā. vari atikarittuk kāṇappaṭuvatuṇṭu. uṭaliṉ evvitamāṉa nōyiṉ aṟikuṟiyum iṉṟi, inta vitamāka, ilēcāka uṭaliṉ veppam atikarippatuṇṭu. kuṭalil pūcci, kallīral mantamāka iyaṅkutal, toṇṭiyil ciṟitu iraṇam, ivikaḷ tiṉacari veppa niliyic caṟṟu uyarttik kāṭṭakkūṭum.

kuḻantikaḷukkuk kāyccal uṇṭupaṇṇum nōykaḷ:

1. caḷippu. 2. toṇṭi aṭippāṉ. 3. iṉpuḷuyaṉcā. 4. mūḷi uṟi aḻaṟci (Menngitis) 5. taṭṭammi (Measles). 6. ceṅkāyccal (Scarietiever). 7. maṉṉik kaṭṭi (Mumps). 8. vicūāi (Small pox) 9. kakkuvāṉ irumal. (Wooping Cough) 10. mūccukkuḻalaṟci (Bronchtis). 11. nimōṉiyā, 12. toṇṭic catippuṇ vīkkam. 13. ṭipāyṭu kāyccal. 14. ṭipas kāyccal. 15. cītapēti (Dysentry) 16. ciṟu nīrakattil nōy. (Nephritis). 17. kallīral nōykaḷ. 18, muṭakkuvātam (Rheumatism) 19. kṣayam. 20. cīḻkkaṭṭikaḷ, coṟi ciraṅku. 21. iḷampiḷḷi vātam mutaliyaṉa.

potuvāka mutal vayatiliruntu aintu vayatuvari kuḻantikkuk cātāraṇa caḷippu, mūccuk kuḻalaṟci pōṉṟa nōykaḷāl cila camayam, kāyccal 1040 pā. vari ēṟi viṭuvatuṇṭu. toṇṭi aṭippāṉ, nimōṉiyā pōṉṟa apāyakaramāṉa nōykaḷil kāyccal 1010 pā. vukku mēlē kāṇappaṭāmal iruppatuṇṭu. ākavē kāyccal aḷavik koṇṭu nōyiṉ taṉmiyi uṇarvatu takunta muṟiyalla. kuḻantip paruvattil narampu maṇṭalam uṟutiyaṭiyāta niliyil iruppatiṉāl uṭampil tiṭīreṉṟu ativeppa ēṟpaṭṭāl narampu maṇṭalam pātikkappaṭṭu kacivu ēṟpaṭṭuviṭum. ākavē atit taṭukka nōykaḷik caṭṭeṉat taṇikkum upāyaṅkaḷik kiyāḷvatu takunta muṟiyākum. kuḻantiyiṉ taliyil ais piyi vittuk kāyccalit taṇiyac ceyyavēṇṭum. oru vayatuṟku oru kireyiṉ eṭi eṉṟa aḷavil aintu vayatiṟkuṭpaṭṭa kuḻantikku, aintu kireyiṉ aḷaviṟku mēṟpaṭātapaṭi āspariṉ koṭuttāl, kāyccal caṭṭeṉat taṇintu viṭum. kāyccal vēṟu pala nōykaḷiṉ veḷippaṭiyāṉa ciṉṉamātalāl anta nōykaḷukkut takuntapaṭi cikicci ceyvatu avaciyam.

maṉṉikkaṭṭi (Mumps) : itip poṉṉukku vīṅki eṉṟum, puṭṭālammi eṉṟum aḻippārkaḷ. itu toṟṟu nōy.

nōykkuk kāraṇam: oru vita viras kirumikaḷ, umiḻ nīrc curappikaḷip pātippatiṉāl avikaḷ vīṅkukiṉṟaṉa. accurappikaḷil parōṭṭīṭu (Parotid) eṉṟa kaṉṉac curappi mukkiyamāka pātikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.

nōyk kuṟi: toṟṟu ēṟpaṭṭa piṉ, cumār 17 allatu 21 nāṭkaḷukkup piṟakutāṉ in nōy kuḻantiyiṭam kāṇappeṟum. vāy, kaṇ, mūkku vaḻiyē kirumikaḷ uḷḷē paravic celkiṉṟaṉa. kuḷir kāyccal, talivali, vānti, ivaṟṟuṭaṉ nōy toṭaṅkalām. allatu kīḻttāṭiyiṉ piṉpakkam ilēcāka valikkat tuvaṅkip, piṟaku kāyccal, talivali, vāntiyuṭaṉ toṭaṅkalām. umiḻnīrc curappiyiṉ vīkkam mutalil ētāvatu oru pakkattil toṭaṅkum. ruempālum iṭappakkattuc curappi mutalil vīṅkat tuvaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. iṭatu kātiṉ aṭiyil kīḻttāṭiyiṉ piṉpuṟattil ciṟitaḷavāka oru vīkkam tōṉṟat toṭaṅkic, cumār mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷil parōṭṭīṭu curappiyum, cila camayam iraṇṭu pakkattu pījaṅkaḷum vīṅkip pōvatuṇṭu. itit tavira, āṇ kuḻantikaḷukku mārpu vīkkamum, nōvum ēṟpaṭuvatuṇṭu. itēpōl peṇ kuḻantikaḷukku cūlpiyil vīkkam ēṟpaṭṭu vayiṟṟu nōyi uṇṭu paṇṇalām. ciṟu nīrakaṅkaḷ pātikkappaṭṭu, iṭuppil vali ēṟpaṭuvatum uṇṭu. peṇ kuḻantikaḷukkum mārpakam vīṅki nōvu koṭuppatuṇṭu. ivikaḷit tavirak kaṇiyam, mūḷi, kātu, kaṇ, mūṭṭukkaḷ, itayam, ciṟu nīrakaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa enta uṟuppum inta nōyāl pātikkappaṭakkūṭum. pījaṅkaḷi pātippatiṉāl nōyāḷi piṟkālattil malaṭākap pōkalām.

cikicci : itaṟkeṉa taṉippaṭṭa maruntukaḷ illi. paṭukkiyil ōyvāka irukka vēṇṭum. vīkkattiṟkuc cūṭāṉa ottaṭam pōṭavēṇṭum. nōvu kuṟiya, āspariṉ pōṉṟa maruntukaḷ ētēṉum koṭukkalām. pijattil vīkkam ēṟpaṭṭāl avikaḷip pañciṉāl cuṟṟi, vīkkattiṉ kaṉam, avikaḷ mītu uṟuttā vaṇṇam pātukākkavēṇṭum. vīkkam kuṟiya ais kaṭṭikaḷi avikaḷ mītu vikkalām. piṟkālattil ik kuḻantikaḷ malaṭākātirukka muṉ kūṭṭiyē innōyiṉ pōtu ṭietil sṭilpōsṭirāl (Diety stiboestrol) eṉum maruntik koṭukkum muṟiyic cila vittiyarkaḷ kiyāṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. nōyiṉpoḻutu vāyic cuttamāka aṉṟāṭam kaḻuvi varavēṇṭum. nōyiṉ poḻutu pāl, pārli, nīr, paḻaccāṟu, pōṉṟa uṇavik koṭukka vēṇṭum. nōyiṉāl ēṟpaṭum maṟṟiya kōḷāṟṟiṟkut takkapaṭi cikicci ceyvatu muṟiyākum. vīkkam vāṅkiya piṉpu, kuḻanti cumār mūṉṟu vāra kālam maṟṟak kuḻantikaḷuṭaṉ cērntu viḷiyāṭātiruppatu nōyip pāvātirukkac ceyyum vaḻi.



mūḷi uṟi aḻaṟci (Menngitis) : 1. pōli mūḷi uṟi aḻaṟci. 2. mūḷi uṟi aḻaṟci eṉa iruvakippaṭum.

pōli mūḷi uṟi aḻaṟci : nimōṉiyā, periyammi pōṉṟa koṭiya nōykaḷiṉ pōtu, kirumikaḷāl uṟpattiyākum nañcu, mūḷiyiyum pātippatāl, mūḷi caṉṉi koṇṭa niliyil, mantamākak kāṇappaṭum. innili atika nēram nīṭikkātu, tāṉē kuṟintu cariyāki viṭum. ākavē itip pōli mūḷi uḷi aḻaṟci eṉṟu aḻippatu poruttamākum.

mūḷi uṟi aḻaṟci nōyyikuṟikaḷ : iti mūṉṟu niliyil kāṇalām. mutal nili : talivali, vānti, malaccikkaluṭaṉ, cila camayam toṭaṅkum. cila camayaṅkaḷil kāyccal, vānti, talivali, valippu eṉa varum. iraṇṭām nili : mutal niliyil toṭaṅkiya tali nōvu mikavum atikamākik kuḻanti alaṟum. itic caṉṉi vēkam eṉṟu aḻippatu vaḻakkam. ciṟu kuḻantikaḷukkut tali ūcciyil uḷḷa kuḻi maṟintu veḷiyē vīkkampōl uppik kāṇappaṭum. kāyccal inniliyil mikavum atikamāka irukkum. kaṇkaḷil uḷḷa pāvi ciṟuttu kāṇappaṭum. kaḻuttiṉ piṉpuṟamuḷḷa tacikaḷum, mutukut taṇṭum kaṭiṉamākak kāṇappaṭum. kikālkaḷil uḷḷa tacikaḷ tuṭikkum. mūṉṟām nili : mūḷiyil ēṟpaṭum raṇattilirunta kaciyum nīriṉ tēkkattiṉāl, mūḷi amukkappaṭuvatiṉālum, mūḷi nīr naṉṟāp parava ōṭamuṭiyātu tēkkappaṭuvatiṉālum, pakkavātam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. mūḷiyil, entap pākam pātikkappaṭukiṉṟatō, ataṟkut takkapaṭi, innōyiṉ kuṟikaḷ teṉpaṭum. oṉṟarikkaṇ, muḻukkuruṭu, ceviṭu, mukattu narampu pātikkappaṭal, pakkavātam mutaliyaṉa ﻿ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aḻaṟci mikavum kaṭumiyākippōṉāl kāyccal mikavum atikamāki, nāṭittuṭippuk kuṟintu, mūccut tāṟumāṟāka veḷippaṭat tuvaṅki, niṉivu kulintu, muṭivil maraṇam uṇṭākiṟatu.

cikicci : perumpālum kuḻanti in nōyiṉiṉṟu kuṇamaṭivatu apūrvam. mūḷiyil ēṟpaṭṭirukkum aḻuttattik kuṟikka aṭikkaṭi mutukut taṇṭil ūciyiṉāl kutti nīr eṭukka vēṇṭum potu vāka kṣayarōkattiṟkut tarukiṟa sṭirepṭṭōmiciṉ pōṉṟa maruntukaḷik kiyāḷavēṇṭum. maṟṟak kirumikaḷāl ēṟpaṭum nōykku, calpāṉilamiṭu vakikaḷ, peṉiciliṉ pōṉṟa maruntukaḷ payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. inta nōy vanta kuḻantikaḷi maruttuvac cāliyil vittuc cikicci ceyvatu nalam.

naṭakka muṭiyāmi : pattāvatu mātattil eḻuntiruntu, cuvarip piṭittukkoṇṭu naṭakkat toṭaṅkum kuḻanti oṉṟari vayatiṟkuḷ naṉṟāka naṭakkat toṭaṅkiviṭum. cila kuḻantikaḷ vayatu mūṉṟu ākiyum naṭavā.

kāraṇam 1. kaṇinōy: inta nōyil elumpu melintu pōvatiṉāl, kuḻanti tāmatamāka naṭakkap paḻakukiṟatu. 2. mūḷi mantam. 3. pārica vāyuviṉāl kāl pātikkappaṭṭiruttal. 4. piṟakkum pōtu, iṭuppup pūṭṭu naḻuviyiruttal. 5. pala nāṭkaḷ nōyākap paṭuttirunta kuḻantikku naṭappatu maṟantupōy viṭutalumuṇṭu. 6. kṣayam, muṭakku vātam mutaliyanōykaḷ, muṭṭikaḷip pātippatuṇṭu.

cikicci : kaṇi nōy vanta kuḻantikaḷukku, ūṭṭamāṉa uṇavu, kālciyam, viṭṭamiṉ ṭī mutaliyavi atikam aḷittāl virivil elumpu uṟutiyaṭintu, naṭakkat tuvaṅkum. iḷampiḷḷi vātattiṉāl naṭakka muṭiyātu pōkum kuḻantikaḷukku, uriya kālattil taṉippaṭṭa cikicci aḷittāl, oraḷavu avikaḷukku naṭakkum tiṟamiyi ēṟpaṭuttalām.

pēca muṭiyāmi : mūṉṟu vayatiṟkuḷ kuḻantikaḷukku anēkamākap pēcum tiṟami naṉku ēṟpaṭṭu viṭuvatuṇṭu. cila kuḻantikaḷ vayatu mūṉṟu ākiyum, ciṟitum pēca muyalvatilli. kāraṇam : 1. piṟaviyilē ceviṭṭu, ūmi. 2. mūḷi mantam. 3. mūkkaṭiccati; toṇṭi cati ivikaḷ vīṅki, raṇamāka iruttal. 4. nākkuk kaṭṭu (tongue tie)

cikicci : kāraṇattik kavaṉittu ēṟṟa cikicci ceyvatu nalam.

alerji : itu nuriyīral, mūkkiṉ uṭpuṟattōl, ivikaḷi pātittāl, kācam, vaṟṟāta caḷi ivikaḷ ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tōliṉ mītu kāṇappaṭum poḻutu, ekcimā eṉṉum civanta taṭippukkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. kuṭalip pātittāl, vāntiyum pētiyumāka veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu. muṭṭikaḷip pātikkiyil muṭṭiyil vīkkam ēṟpaṭum.

kuḻantikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum kampaḷic caṭṭikaḷ cila camayam, cila kuḻantikaḷukku ekcimā eṉum caruma nōy uṇṭupaṇṇukiṉṟatu. itit tavira, uṭkoḷḷum uṇavu cila cantarppaṅkaḷil alerjiyi uṇṭupaṇṇuvatuṇṭu. muṭṭi, pāl, ārañcup paḻaṅkaḷ, mīṉ, kōḻi iṟicci, cila tāṉiyaṅkaḷ, veṇṇey mutaliyavi, kuḻantikaḷukkuk koḷḷāmal, alerji nōyi uṇṭu paṇṇuvatuṇṭu. apūrvamākac cila kuḻantikaḷukku tāyppāl ottukkoḷvatilli. piṟanta kuḻanti, tāyppāl cāppiṭat tuvaṅki, iraṇṭu nāṭkaḷil carumattiṉ mītu civappāṉa ciṟu puḷḷikaḷ pōṉṟa eḻuccikaḷ kāṇappuṭum. itic cevvāppu eṉac cātāraṇamāka aḻippārkaḷ. tāyppāl ottuk koḷḷātu ēṟpaṭum alerji nōykkut tāyiṉ pāli niṟuttiviṭṭu, vēṟu ceyaṟki uṇavu koṭukkavēṇṭum. allatu tāyppāl ottuk koḷḷumpaṭi, uṭal niliyi apivirutti ceyyattakka muṟikaḷ (Desentisation) kiyāḷa vēṇṭum. tāyppāli niṟuttiviṭṭu, puṭṭippāl aḷippataṟkup patil tamiḻnāṭṭu vaḻakkappaṭik kaḻutip pāl aḷittuk kuḻantiyiṉ uṭal niliyi apivirutti ceyyalām.

piṟavik kiranti (Congeental syphils): kiranti nōy uḷḷa peṟṟōrkaḷukkup piṟakkum kuḻanti, annōyuṭaṉēyē piṟakkiṉṟatu. pārkka : kiranti.

narampu nōykaḷ : kuḻantiyiṉ narampu maṇṭalam, periyavarkaḷiṉ narampumaṇṭalattip pōṉṟu muṟṟu peṟṟu uṟutiyaṭiyātu. vaḷarcci niliyil iruppatāl eḷitil pātikkappaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl palvēṟu narampuk kōḷāṟukaḷ kuḻantikaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭuvatuṇṭu.

kāraṇam : 1. piṟavi cila kuḻantikaḷ piṟaviyilēyē mikavum payanta iyalpuṭiyavarkaḷāka iruppārkaḷ. cattam, ataṭṭal mutaliyavi kēṭṭatum naṭunaṭuṅkip pōvatuṇṭu. 2. kaṇinōy, muṭakku vātam pōṉṟa nōykaḷ.

mukattaci acivu : itu taṉippaṭṭa oru nōy alla. kuḻantiyiṉ narampu maṇṭalam mikavum melinta niliyil iruppati, veḷippaṭiyākak kāṭṭum ōr ﻿aṟikuṟi. mukattil, kaṉṉattiṉ mītu mellat taṭṭiṉāl, aṅkuḷḷa muka narampu atircciyuṟṟuk kaṉṉattut taci tuṭikkat tuvaṅkum. inta aṟikuṟi teṉpaṭṭāl, narampumaṇṭalam mikavum melinta niliyil uḷḷatu eṉa aṟintu, takka cikicci ceyyavēṇṭum.

mānta irippu (Laryngsmus strdulus) : ) itu pal muḷikkum paruvattil kāṇappaṭum. perumpālum itaṟku ilakkākum kuḻantikaḷ, ēṟkeṉavē kaṇiya nōyiṉāl pātikkappaṭṭiruppārkaḷ.

nōykkuṟi : kuralvaḷi vāyil (Giots) icivukaṇṭu varuvatāl nuriyīralukkuk kāṟṟuppōka muṭiyātu taṭaṅkal ēṟpaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟatu. ataṉāl mūccu naṉṟākac cuvācikka iyalātu, tiṇaṟal ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. viḷiyāṭikkoṇṭirunta kuḻanti tiṭīreṉṟu cuvācikka muṭiyātu, taliyip piṉpakkam cāyttukkoṇṭu, mukam nīlamākit tiṭīreṉṟu koḷakoḷaveṉṟu vinōtamāṉa oliyuṇṭākkum. ciṟitu poḻutu etiyō kaṇṭu payantatu pōl aḻutuviṭṭup, piṟaku maṟupaṭiyum viḷiyāṭṭil īṭupaṭum. itu mikavum apāyakaramāṉatoru kōḷāṟu. cila camayam mūccut tiṇaṟal atikamāki viṭṭāl, kuḻanti maraṇamaṭivatum uṇṭu. itit tāṉ māntam eṉkiṟārkaḷ.

cikicci : iciviṉpōtu mukattil kuḷirnta nīrit teḷikkavēṇṭum. kaḻuttiṉ mītu cūṭāṉa ottaṭam koṭuppatum naṉmi payakkum. icivu mikavum mummuramāka iruntāl, ciṟitu kuḷōrōpāram mayakkam koṭuppatum atittaṭukkum oru muṟiyām. ittakiya icivu varātu taṭuppatutāṉ nalla cikicciyākum. intak kuḻantikaḷi aṭittut tuyarappaṭutti, maṉac caṅkaṭam uṇṭākkātu pātukākkavēṇṭum. narampu maṇṭalatti ﻿nitāṉappaṭutta, purōmiṭukaḷ, kuḷōrāl mutaliya maruntukaḷ uḷḷukkuc cāppiṭak koṭuppatu nalam. kaṇinōy mūlakāraṇamāka iruntāl, ataṟkut takunti cikiccikaḷ ceyvatu tāṉ muṟiyākum. mīṉ eṇṇey, ārañcup paḻaccāṟu, muṭṭi, irumpuc cattuḷḷa ṭāṉikkukaḷ, ivikaḷik koṭuttup, potuvāka uṭal niliyi nalamuṟa vittiruntāl, aṭikkaṭi icivu varātu kāppāṟṟalām.

piṟavik kuṟaḷvaḷi naṭukkam (congenital Laryngeal stridor) : ituvum narampupaṟṟiya oru kōḷāṟu. pēccuk kuḻalkaḷil uḷḷa taci māṟupaṭṭu iyaṅkuvatāl, kuḻanti kāṟṟi uḷḷukku iḻuttuc cuvācikkiyil, kural nāṇkaḷ (Vocal chords) caṭṭeṉa virintu koṭuttuk kāṟṟi uṭpuṟam aṉuppāmal ciṟitu tāmatikkiṉṟaṉa.

nōykkuṟi: piṟanta cila vāraṅkaḷil, kuḻanti mūccu viṭukiyil pūṉi cīṟukiṉṟatip pōṉṟa oru oli eḻum. āṉāl, tūṅkukiyil ittakiya oli kāṇappaṭātu. kuḻanti kōpappaṭum poḻutum, ericcalāka irukkiyilum, inta oli mikavum atikamākak kēṭkum.

cikicci : itu manta icivippōl, koṭiyatalla. itu kuḻantiyiṉ mutal vayatil tāṉē maṟintupōkum. ākavē taṉippaṭṭa cikicci tēviyilli.

ki kāl icivu (Tetany) : itu, kikaḷilum kālkaḷilum uḷḷa cila tacikaḷ tiṭīreṉṟu iḻuttuk koḷvatiṉāl, ēṟpaṭum icivu nōy.

kāraṇam : in nōykku mūlakāraṇamāka uḷḷatu, kaṇinōyum, atika pētiyum ākum. oru vayatiṟku ﻿mēṟpaṭṭa kuḻantiyiṭam kaṇinōy karṇap paṭukiyil, inta icivu nōyiyum varak kāṇalām. oru vayatiṟkuṭpaṭṭa kuḻanti, pētiyil kaṣṭappaṭukiyil ittakiya icivum tōṉṟalām. āṉāl inta icivuṟku mukkiyak kāraṇam, irattiluḷḷa kālciya aḷaviṟku veku kuṟivāka iruppatu. irattattil kālciyam kaṟintu pōvataṟkuk kāraṇam kēṭayac curappittuṇi (Prathyroikd) eṉum curappi oḻuṅkīṉamāka vēli ceyvatutāṉ. in nōy uyirukkuk keṭuti ceyyātu, eṉiṉum mikavum vali koṭukkum nōy.

cikicci : kaṇi nōykku ukanta cikicci ceytal, vēṇṭum. kālciyam lākṭēṭṭu maruntu, tiṉacari 15 liruntu 30 kireyiṉ koṭuppatum nalamākum. attuṭaṉ mīṉ eṇṇey tiṉacari oru tēkkaraṇṭi aḷavu, kāliyilum māliyilum koṭukka vēṇṭum. icivu nōyik kuṟikka, purōmiṭukaḷ, kuḷōrāl pōṉṟa maruntukaḷ, appōtikkappōtu koṭuppatu nalam.

tali āṭṭam: taliyāṭṭum pommi pōṉṟu, kuḻanti mēlum kīḻum allatu ippuṟamum appuṟamumākat taliyacittuk koṇṭiruppatu, oru vita narampuk kōḷāṟākum. ittuṭaṉ cila camayam, kaṇkaḷiṉ viḻikaḷum kaṭiyārap peṇṭulam pōl āṭuvatum uṇṭu.

itu cumār oru vayatiṟkuṭpaṭṭa kuḻantikaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭum. kuḻanti, etiyāvatu oru poruḷi ūṉṟip pārkka muyaṉṟavuṭaṉ, tali kaṭikārap peṇṭulam pōl āṭat tuvaṅkiviṭum. narampu maṇṭalam oḻuṅkāka iyaṅkum kacti peṟātatiṉāl, ēṟpaṭum itu, virivil tāṉē kuṇamākiviṭum. ākavē cikicci tēviyilli.



﻿talimōtal : mēlē kūṟappaṭṭa taliyāṭṭam pōl, kuḻanti tiṭīreṉṟu aṭikkaṭi taliyik kaṭṭil, cuvar, peṭṭi pōṉṟa kaṭiṉap poruḷkaḷ mītu tāṉē ceṉṟu mōtik koḷvatum, narampuk kōḷāṟukaḷil oru vakiyākum. cila camayam koñcam aḻuttamāka mōtikkoḷvatiṉāl ciṟu kāyam ētēṉum ēṟpaṭuvatum uṇṭu.

kāraṇam : muḷikkum pal vētaṉi ceyvatiṉālum, kātil vali iruntālum, kuḻantiyiṉ narampukaḷ vētaṉi koḷvatāl, atit tavirkka, avvāṟu mōtikkoḷkiṟatu. taṉippaṭṭa cikicci tēviyilli. purōmiṭu maruntukaḷ ciṟitaḷavu avvappōtu aḷittāl kuṇamākiviṭum.

iravil payakkōḷāṟu (Nightterrors) : iravil tūṅkum kuḻanti, caṭakkeṉṟu viḻittukkoṇṭu, vīṟiṭṭu alaṟi naṭuṅkuvatum, oruvaki narampuk kōḷāṟākum. aṭikkaṭi ippaṭi nērntatāl kuḻantiyiṉ narampu maṇṭalam mikavum melintu pōkakkūṭum. ataṉāl tūkkam keṭum. uṭal nalam aḻintupōkum. payak kōḷāṟukaḷukkuk kuḻanti ilakkāvataṟku uṇmiyāṉa kāraṇam, uṭalnalam paḻutupaṭṭiruppatu tāṉ. malaccikkal, toṇṭiccati aṭiṉāyṭu mutaliyavi vīṅki vaḷarntu viṭutal, ivikaḷ kāraṇamākiṉṟaṉa.

cikicci : toṇṭiyil ēṟpaṭṭirukkum toṇṭic cati vaḷarccikkut, takunta kātu, mūkku, toṇṭi maruttuvariṭam kāṭṭi ālōcaṉi peṟuvatum uttamam. malaccikkalukku orumuṟi pētikkuk koṭuppatu nalam. iravil iruḷāka irukkum aṟiyil, taṉiyē kuḻantiyit tūṅkaviṭak kūṭātu. aṟikkuḷ ciṟu viḷakki erippatu naṉmiyākum. vayiṟu niṟiya uṇavu koṭuttu uṭaṉē tūṅkac ceyyātu, ciṟitu nēram viḷiyāṭṭuk kāṭṭi, uṇavu cīraṇamaṭiyat tuvaṅkiya piṉpu tūṅkac ceyvatu naṉmi payakkum.

paṟkaḷik kaṭittal : tūkkattil paṟkaḷik kaṭippatum uṇṭu. itaṟku acīraṇam, kuṭal pūccikaḷ mutaliyavi kāraṇam. cikicci aṅkaṅkē vivarikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

paṭukkiyil ciṟunīrp pōkku : ciṟunīrp pikkut taṉṉuḷ ciṟunīri aṭakki vittukkoṇṭu, icci ēṟpaṭum poḻutu maṭṭum kaḻikkum taṉittiṟami, kuḻantikku mūṉṟu vayatiṟkumēl, anēkamāka intac cakti, ellāk kuḻantikaḷukkum ēṟpaṭṭu viṭuvatiṉāl, niṉitta poḻutu ciṟunīrik kaṇṭa iṭamellām kaḻikkātu kaṭṭuppaṭuttum cakti ēṟpaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟatu. āṉāl, cila kuḻantikaḷukku ciṟunīrp piyik kaṭṭuppaṭutta iyaluvatilli. ik kuḻantikaḷ iravil uṟaṅkumpōtu ciṟunīr kaḻittuviṭum.

kāraṇam mūṉṟuvakippaṭṭatu :1. (a) kīrippūcci (Thread worm) kuṭalil iruppatu. ivi iravu vēḷiyil ācaṉa vāyil vantu taṅkikkoṇṭu arippi uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa. (b) toṇṭiyilum mūkkilum cati vaḷarntiruppatu. (c) peṇ kuḻantikaḷ maṇalil viḷiyāṭip peṇ uṟuppil puṇ ēṟpaṭuvatu. āṇ kuḻantikaḷukku āṇ uṟuppiṉ muṉtōl (Peptice) ciṟunīr tuvāratti mūṭum aḷavu nīṇṭu vaḷarntiruppatu. ciṟunīril amilam atikamāka iruppatu mutaliyaṉa. 2. ciṟunīr peyyap payiṟci aḷiyātiruppatu. 3. payam, kōpam, varuttam, atircci pōṉṟavi.



﻿cikicci : puṇ, pūccit tontaravu ivikaḷik kavaṉittu, uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ cikicci ceytal vēṇṭum. ciṟu paruvattil, atāvatu oru vayatiliruntu kuḻantiyi iravil oru muṟi naṭuvil eḻuppic ciṟunīr kaḻikkac ceytu, payiṟci aḷittal vēṇṭum. iravil eḻuppum nērattiyum, koñcam koñcamākat taḷḷippōṭṭuk koṇṭuvantāl, muṭivil iravu muḻuvatum eḻuppāviṭiṉum, kuḻanti ciṟunīrip paṭukkiyil kaḻiyāmal, viṭintu eḻunta piṉpu, tāṉē veḷiyē ceṉṟu kaḻikkat tuvaṅkum. payam, kōpam mutaliya uṇāccikaḷiṉ kāraṇamāka ēṟpaṭṭatāka iruntāl, taṉippaṭṭa cikicci tēviyilli. makiḻcciyāṉa maṉanili, tāyiṉ āṟutal moḻikaḷ, mutaliyaviyē kuḻantiyiṭam kāṇappaṭum ip paḻakkatti nīkkiviṭum.

aṅka cēṣṭikaḷ : mukattic curukkutal, kaṇkaḷi imittal, mūkki uṟiñcutal, kikālkaḷi ippaṭiyum, appaṭiyum acittal pōṉṟa kāraṇamaṟṟa, arttamaṟṟa ceyalkaḷ, kuḻantikaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭuvatu uṇṭu. ituvum narampu paṟṟiya oru kōḷāṟutāṉ.

kāraṇam : muṭakkuvāta nōyiṉāl pīṭikkappaṭṭu melintupōtal, iratta cōki, paḷḷikkūṭattil atika vēli, potuvāka, nalakkuṟivu mutaliyaṉa ākum. itit tavira, cottip pal, toṇṭiyil iraṇam, kaṇkaḷil pārvik kōḷāṟu mutaliyavikaḷum kāraṇamākiṉṟaṉa.

cikicci : ōyvu, nalla uṇavu, ivaṟṟuṭaṉ toṇṭi raṇam, kaṇ pārvik kōḷāṟu ivikaḷukkut taṉippaṭṭa cikicci ceytāl, intak kōḷāṟu nīṅkum. inta mātiri aṅka cēṣṭikaḷi niṟutta muyala vēṇṭumeṉṟu tāyār mellak kuḻantikaḷukku eṭuttuc collavatum nalamākum.



﻿mūrcci : paḷḷip pavaruttil kuḻantikaḷukku ēṟpaṭum narampu campantamāṉa nōy.

nōykkuṟi : viḷiyāṭikkoṇṭirukkum kuḻanti, tiṭīreṉṟu mukam veḷuttu, veṟittup pārtta niliyil tiṭīreṉak kīḻa viḻuntu mūrcci aṭintu viṭum. niṉivu pūraṇamāka maṅki viṭuvatilli. cila camayam vānti, kumaṭṭal ivikaḷum kāṇappaṭum. uṇarvu pāti tavaṟiya innili, cila vināṭikaḷiruntu arimaṇi nēram variyum nīṭittirukkum.

kāraṇam : iyalpākavē payanta kuṇam koṇṭa maṉanili, acīraṇam, potuvāṉa ārōkkiya nalivu.

cikicci : narampukaḷit tūṇṭiviṭum sṭrikṉīṉ kalanta ṭāṉikkukaḷ, nalla ōyvu, iṭamāṟṟam, ūṭṭamāṉa uṇavu.

iḻuppu (Convulsion): iru narampumaṇṭalam paṟṟiya nōykaḷil mikavum kaṭumiyāṉa vakiyic cērntatu.

pārkka : iḻuppu.

hisṭiriyā: kuḻantikaḷukku hisṭiriyā varuvataṟku iraṇṭu mukkiyak kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. 1. nōy ēṟpaṭa atika kāraṇamāka irunta cūḻnili. 2. nōyit tūṇṭi uṟpatti ceypavi. mutalāvatāka, mikavum meṉmiyāṉa taṉmiyum uṇarccikaḷ tāṅka muṭiyāta melinta niliyumuṭiya peṟṟōrkaḷukkuk piṟakkum kuḻantikaḷ hisṭiriyā nōykku ilakkākiṉṟaṉa. in nōyit tūṇṭi varac ceyyak kāraṇamāka amivaṉa ciṟu kāyam allatu payam mutaliyavikaḷ ākum.

kuḻantikaḷiṭam hisṭīriyā palavakit tōṟṟattil kāṇappaṭum. kikālkaḷi nīṭṭi maṭikka ﻿muṭiyāmi, vāta nōyippōl kāṇappaṭuvatu, kikālkaḷi maṭakkikoṇṭu nīṭṭa muṭiyāmi, ūmi, irumal, vikkal, vīriṭṭu alaṟi mūrcciyuṟuvatu pōṉṟa, palavitamāṉa aṟikuṟikaḷuṭaṉ tōṉṟalām. iti hisṭīriyā tāṉ eṉat tīrmāṉamākak kaṇṭupiṭippataṟku maruttuvarkaḷāl tāṉ iyalum.

cikicci : cūḻnili māṟṟam ukanta cikicci, atikak kaṇṭippō, calukiyō kāṭṭātu, kuḻantiyiṉ nōyip paṟṟic ciṟitu pārāmukamāka iruntāl, nōyippaṟṟik kuḻanti maṟantuviṭat tuvaṅkum. naṭakka muṭiyātapaṭi vāta nōyal paṭuttuk kiṭakkum kuḻantiyit takunta maruttuvarikkoṇṭu cikicci ceyvatu nalam.





kuḻantip paḷḷi : cātāraṇamāka 2 mutal 5 vayatu allatu 6 vayatu varayuaiḷḷa kuḻantikaḷukkāka ēṟpaṭṭatu kuḻantip paḷḷiyākum. āṉāl, cila nāṭukaḷil 7 vayatu variyuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷiyum ip paḷḷikaḷil cērttuk koḷkiṟārkaḷ.

kuḻantip paḷḷiyāṉatu, kuḻantik kāppu viṭutikkum, ārampap paḷḷikkum iṭiyē, oru mukkiya niliyamāka iruntu varukiṟatu.

nōkkam : kuḻantikaḷuṭiya uṭal vaḷarccikkum, uḷa vaḷarccikkum ēṟṟa cūḻniliyi amittuk koṭuppatum, ac cūḻniliyil āciriyarkaḷiṉ mēṟpārviyil kuḻantikaḷ payamiṉṟic cuyēcciyāka vāḻac ceyvatum, payamillāmal vāḻac ceyvataṉ mūlam uṇmi pēcum vaḻakkatti vaḷarppatum, naṟ paḻakkaṅkaḷi uṇṭākkuvatum, uṭal vaḷarccikkāṉa ūṭṭattiyum, payiṟciyiyum aḷippatum, kuḻantip paḷḷiyiṉ mukkiya nōkkaṅkaḷākum.



﻿vāḻkki aṉupavaṅkaḷē kalviyākum eṉpatu kuḻantip paḷḷikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭik koḷki. pāṭam collik koṭuppatu eṉpataṟku iṅku iṭam kiṭiyātu. ārāytal, kavaṉittal, cōtaṉi ceytal, pārttuc ceytal, mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku aṉukūlamāṉa cūḻniliyi ēṟpaṭuttiviṭṭāl, avaṟṟāl uṇṭākum aṉupavaṅkaḷē kuḻankiḷukkuk kalviyāki viṭukiṉṟaṉa. atuvē kuḻantikaḷ kalvi peṟuvataṟkāṉa iyaṟki muṟiyākum.

varalāṟu : iṅkilāntu nāṭṭil niyūlāṉark eṉṉumiṭattil rāparṭ oyiṉ eṉpavar, 1816-il mutaṉ mutalākac cicup paḷḷi (Infant school) eṉpati niṟuviṉār.ittakiya paḷḷi avaciyam eṉṉum karuttuc ciṟitu ciṟitāka vaḷarntu vantatu. 1907-il aracāṅkak kalvi ilākā niyamitta kamiṭṭiyār, ittakiya paḷḷiyik kuḻantikaḷ paḷḷi (Nursery School) eṉṟu aḻikkalāyiṉar.

1944-il āṅkila aracāṅkak kalvic caṭṭamāṉatu, ittakiya paḷḷikaḷ niṟuvik kalviyaḷippatu aracāṅkattiṉ kaṭamikaḷil oṉṟākac ceytatu. 1946-il 2-5 vayatu variyuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷukkāṉa paḷḷikaḷ 97-m piṟa paḷḷikaḷil, ik kuḻantikaḷukkāṉa vakuppukkaḷ 1828-m iruntaṉa.

jermeṉiyil purōpel eṉpavar, kiṇṭar kārṭṭaṉ (Kinder garden) eṉṟa peyaril oruvakik kuḻantip paḷḷiyi, 1837-il amittār. ittāliyil māṉṭicōri ammiyār oruvakik kuḻantip paḷḷiyi 1907-l amittār. āṅkila muṟi māṉṭicōri muṟiyi (ta. ka.) viṭa, kiṇṭar kārṭṭaṉ muṟiyē atikamākat taḻuviyuḷḷatu.



﻿intiyāvil, ceṉṟa irupatu āṇṭukaḷāka inta mūṉṟu vakip paḷḷikaḷuṭaṉ kāntiyaṭikaḷ amitta pūrvātārap paḷḷikaḷ (ta. ka.) eṉṟa vakik kuḻantip paḷḷikaḷum tōṉṟivarukiṉṟaṉa. e.ca.

kiṇṭar kārṭṭaṉ muṟi : kuḻantikaḷukkuk kalvi kaṟpikkak kiṇṭar kārṭaṉ muṟiyi ēṟpaṭuttiyavar piriṭrik vilhelm ākasṭ purōpel (ta. ka.) eṉṉum jermāṉiyat tattuva ñāṉiyum kalvic cīrttiruttavāyumāvār. ivar vakutta pāṭamuṟikaḷ, kuḻantikaḷiṉ viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷiyē ātāramākak koṇṭiruntaṉa. viḷiyāṭṭiṉ mūlam kaṟpippatiyē ivar mikac ciṟanta muṟiyeṉak kūṟiṉār.

purōpel, 1839-il tammuṭiya putiya kalvi niliyaṅkaḷukkuk kiṇṭar kārṭṭaṉ eṉṉum peyari vaḻaṅkiṉār. itaṟkuk ‘kuḻantit tōṭṭam’ eṉpatu poruḷ. ivar tammuṭiya paḷḷiyi, oru tōṭṭamākavum, piḷḷikaḷit tōṭṭattiluḷḷa ceṭikaḷākavum karutiṉār. tāvaraṅkaḷ vaḷarvataṟku evvāṟu cūriya veppam, nalla kāṟṟu mutaliyavi avaciyamō, avvāṟē kuḻantikaḷiṉ uṭal, uṇarcci, uḷḷam, mutaliyavi vaḷam peṟap paranta iṭam, nalla kāṟṟu, cūriya veḷiccam mutaliyaṉa iṉṟiyamiyātavi eṉṟu avar maṉitaṉukkuriya kalvi (Education of Man) eṉṉum tammuṭiya nūlil eṭutturikkiṟār.

ellāvitak kalviyum, kuḻantiyiṉ cuya muyaṟciyāl tāṉ ēṟpaṭukiṟatu eṉṟa uṇmiyiyum, kuḻanti taṉ uḷḷa niliyit terivikkum paṉmiyē kalviyiṉ aṭippaṭiyāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum tattuvattiyum, kiṇṭar kārṭṭaṉ muṟi vaṟpuṟuttukiṟatu. kuḻantiyāṉatu kalviyi āciriyariṭamiruntu ﻿kaṟṟuk koḷkiṟatu eṉpativiṭṭu, atu tāṉē kalviyik kaṟṟukoḷḷak kūṭiyatu eṉṟu im muṟi karutukiṟatu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, āciriyar kuḻantikku oru katiyic coṉṉāl, kuḻanti ak katiyit taṉ conta vārttiyiṉāl colluvatōṭu, pākkaḷ, cikikaḷ, paṭaṅkaḷ mūlam, kaṭitam, kaḷimaṇ mutaliya poruḷkaḷiṉāl ciṟiya cāmāṉkaḷic ceytu kāṭṭal mūlamum, viḷakkum eṉṟu avar kūṟiṉār.

mutaṉ mutalil ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭa kiṇṭar kārṭṭaṉ paḷḷikaḷil tām vakutta muṟippaṭi kaṟpippataṟkāka, purōpel pattuk kalvik karuvikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉār. avi, purōpel, kalvi muṟikku aḷitta naṉkoṭikaḷ eṉappaṭum. āṉāl, piṟkālattil, ivi, purōpel etirpārtta muṟiyil payaṉpaṭāmal pōṉatāl, ippōtu kiviṭappaṭṭaṉa.

kuḻantikaḷukku oru moḻiyik kaṟpikkum āciriyar, atit tiruttamākavum, piḻiyillāmalum pēca vēṇṭumeṉṟum, āciriyar maḻali moḻiyil pēciṉāl, kuḻanti cariyākap pēcak kaṟṟukkoḷḷātu pōkum eṉṟu purōpel vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟiṉār. katikaḷum, pāṭṭukkaḷum, kuḻantiyiṉ moḻi vaḷattip perukka utavumātalāl, kiṇṭar kārṭṭaṉ paḷḷiyil ivikaḷukkut taṉicciṟappu aḷikkappaṭum.

paḷḷikku veḷiyē tōṭṭattil viḷiyāṭuvatu, iyaṟkiyuṭaṉ neruṅkiya toṭarpik kuḻantikaḷukku uṇṭākkum. kuḻantikaḷ coṟkaḷik kaṟṟup puttaka vāyilāka aṟivu peṟumuṉṉar, poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ paḻaki aṟiyum aṟivip peṟa vēṇṭum eṉpatum, veṟum aṟivi viṭa, aṟivu peṟum muṟiyē mukkiyamāṉatu eṉpatum ik kalvi muṟiyiṉ aṭippaṭit tattuvamākum.



﻿toṭarcciyāṉa muṉṉēṟṟam, taṉmuyaṟci, ākkacceyal, kittoḻil payiṟci, tōṭṭam, kāṭu, vayal mutaliyavaṟṟilum, paḷḷikkūṭat toḻiṟ cāliyilum, vīṭṭiccuṟṟiyuḷḷa iṭattilum, vēli ceytal ākiyavi kiṇṭar kārṭṭaṉ paḷḷiyiṉ ciṟappiyalkaḷākum.

purōpelukkuk kalvi paṟṟiya ip puraṭcik karuttukkaḷ, pesṭlāji (ta. ka. ) eṉpavaruṭiya toṭarpāl tōṉṟiṉa. pesṭalājiyē ik kālattuk kalvimuṟik karuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭamāvār. māṉṭicōri ammiyār (ta. ka. ) pōṉṟa kalvittuṟi vallunarkaḷ, namakku purōpeliṉ kalvik karuttukkaḷiyum, muṟiyiyum putuppittu aḷittirukkiṉṟaṉar.





kuḻantip pāṭal

(Nursery rhymes) : pāṭṭi virumpāta kuḻantiyē illi eṉṟu collalām. paccik kuḻanti tāy pāṭum tālāṭṭup pāṭalilē iṉpam kāṇkiṟatu. avaḷ pāṭuvatik kēṭṭut tāṉum maḻali moḻiyilē eṉṉeṉṉavō kutappukiṟatu. pēcakkūṭiya āṟṟal peṟṟa kuḻanti, ōci iṉpam, canta iṉpamum koṇṭa ciṟiya pāṭalkaḷip pāṭi makiḻkiṟatu. antap pāṭalkaḷukkup poruḷ irukkavēṇṭum eṉpatilli; ōci iṉpam iruntāl pōtum.

akkakkā ūrkkuruvi

ālam paḻampōṭu

tiṉṉap paḻam pōṭu

tirumuṭikkōr pūp pōṭu





eṉṟu, kirāmattuk kuḻantikaḷ pāṭum. ippaṭi ettaṉiyō pāṭalkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟi uṇṭākkiyavarkaḷ yāreṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyātu. entak kālattil avi tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyātu. kuḻantikaḷuṭiya maḻaliyiliruntē, antap pāṭalkaḷukkuc coṟkaḷum, cantaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum.

kuḻantikaḷiṉ pēccilē, cila taṉippaṭṭa coṟkaḷik kāṇalām. cantiraṉ avikaḷukku, cantamāmā ākiviṭum; cōṟu cōcci ākiviṭum. intac coṟkaḷellām kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷilē iṭampeṟṟu viṭum.

kuruvi, kākki, kiḷi mutaliya paṟavikaḷum, māṭu, nāy, kutiri mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷum, kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷilē atikamāka iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. cantiraṉip paṟṟip pāṭuvatilē kuḻantikaḷukkut taṉi iṉpam uṇṭu. kuḻantikaḷuṭiya viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷip paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷum uṇṭu. potuvākak kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷellām avaṟṟiṉ cūḻniliyiyum, aṉupavattiyum, avi kēṭkum katikaḷ mutaliyavikaḷiyum, avaṟṟiṉ viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷiyum paṟṟiyaṉavāka amintirukkum.

poruḷ atikamāka illāta kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷōṭu, poruḷ niṟinta pāṭalkaḷum uṇṭu.

kuḷḷak kuḷḷaṉi

kuṇṭu vayiṟaṉi

veḷḷik kompaṉi

vināyakaṉit toḻu





kuḷḷaṉ eṉṟum, kuṇṭuvayiṟaṉ eṉṟum kikaḷiṉāl kāṭṭi ip pāṭalip pāṭuvatil kuḻantikkut taṉippaṭṭa viruppam irukkiṟatu.

kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷ ulakameṅkum irukkiṉṟaṉa. vēṟu uyarnta ilakkiyam illāta moḻikaḷilum, eḻuttu vaṭivamē kāṇāta moḻikaḷilum kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷ uṇṭu.



﻿āṅkilam pōṉṟa mēṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷiluḷḷa kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷit tokuttu, nūl vaṭivilē veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. avaṟṟiṟku mika aḻakāṉa vaṇṇap paṭaṅkaḷum tīṭṭiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. nīṇṭa kālamāka vantuḷḷa pāṭalkaḷip piṉpaṟṟip pala kaviñarkaḷum, putiya kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷi iyaṟṟiyirukkiṟārkaḷ.

tamiḻ nāṭṭil uḷḷa paḻiya kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷit tokuttuc ciṟanta muṟiyil iṉṉum veḷiyiṭavilli. kavimaṇi tēciya vināyakam piḷḷi (ta.ka.) mutaliya kaviñarkaḷ putiya kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷ iyaṟṟiyirukkiṟārkaḷ.





kuḻanti vaḷarppu

kuḻanti tāyiṉ karuppiyiliruntu, veḷiyē, putiyatōr iṭattukku varukiṉṟatu. atu ituvari, iruḷum, veppamum uḷḷa iṭattil vāḻntu vantatu. ataṉāl kuḻanti piṟantatum, aḻa ārampikkiṟatu. ati naṉṟākap pōrtti iruṭṭāṉa aṟiyil paṭukka vikka vēṇṭum.

kuḻanti piṟantu, iraṇṭu mūṉṟu maṇinēram ceṉṟatum, atik kuḷippāṭṭuvārkaḷ. ataṉpiṉ ati 24 maṇippoḻutu, kāl pakkatti viṭat talippakkam 3-4 aṅkulam tāḻntatākap paṭukka vippārkaḷ. ippaṭic ceytāl kuḻanti piṟakkumpōtu ētēṉum nīrō, caḷiyō kural vaḷiyiṉuḷ cērntiruntāl veḷiyē vantuviṭum.

kuḻantiyi nāḷtōṟum kuḷippāṭṭa vēṇṭum. kuḷirnta kāṟṟup paṭāmal kākkavēṇṭum. kuḻanti irukkumiṭam eppōtum, nalla kāṟṟu ulavuvatāka irukka vēṇṭum. kuḻantiyik kocukkaḷ kaṭikkā vaṇṇam kavaṉittukkaṭkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. kuḻanti uṟaṅkumpōtu, kocuvali pōṭuvatu nallatu.



kuḻantiyiṉ uṭampil ciṟitu nēram iḷaveyil paṭumpaṭi ceytal nallatu. cūriyaṉuṭiya puṟa ūtāk katirkaḷ, kuḻantiyiṭam kaṇinōy varāmal pātukākkum, vantālum kuṇappaṭuttum.

piṟanta kuḻanti, perum pakutippoḻutu uṟaṅkum. uṇavu uṇṇuvataṟkāka maṭṭumē viḻikkum. nāḷ āka āka atikappoḻutu viḻittirukkum. ōrāṇṭu niṟiyum vari, iraṇṭu taṭavi pakalil uṟaṅkum. ataṉ piṉ oru taṭaviyē uṟaṅkum. kuḻantiyi uṟaṅkap paṇṇat toṭṭililiṭṭu āṭṭuvatu tēviyilli. kuṟippiṭṭa poḻutil uṟaṅkap paḻakkiviṭṭāl pōtum. potuvākac ciṟu kuḻantikaḷ pāl uṇṇumpōtē uṟaṅkiviṭum. atu nallatē.

kuḻantikku uṭi kuṟivākap payaṉpaṭutvatē nallatu. kuḷir tākkāmalirukkumporuṭṭu maṭṭumē payaṉpaṭuttavēṇṭum. veppa nāṭkaḷil, uṭiyillāmal iruppatu nalam. kuḷir kālattilum, urōmattālāṉa tuṇikaḷ kuḻantikku nallatilli. at tuṇi kuḻantiyiṉ melliya tōli uṟuttum. kuḻantiyiṉ tuṇikaḷi veḷukkak kāram mikunta cōppukkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttalākātu.

kuḻanti piṟanta cila nāṭkaḷ vari, perum pakutiyāṉa poḻutu uṟaṅkum. kuḷippāṭṭavum pāl koṭukkavum maṭṭum ati eḻuppa vēṇṭum. nāḷtōṟum mūṉṟu taṭavi tāyppāl koṭukkalām. inta nāṭkaḷil, malappōkku maruntu tara vēṇṭiyatilli. iṭiyiṭiyē paruka nīr koṭuttāl pōtumāṉatu. ivvāṟu kuḻanti, tāyiṉ vayiṟṟukku veḷiyē cuvācikkap paḻakiya piṉṉar, pāl parukap paḻakum. paci eḻumpōtu kuḻanti vīriṭṭu aḻum ﻿uṭaṉē eṭuttut tāyppāl koṭuttāl, kuḻanti uṇṇat toṭaṅkum. appōtu atu pacittuṉpam nīṅkappeṟum uṇarcciyi aṟintukoḷkiṟatu. ivvāṟu cila nāṭkaḷ naṭantapiṉ 3-4 maṇikku oru taṭavi pālūṭṭa vēṇṭum.
