rumāṭṭik kāyccalāl ēṟpaṭum itayak kōḷāṟu: vaḷarum paravattil pākṭīriyāk kirumi kāraṇamāka, vaṟaṭṭu irumal ēṟpaṭṭu, piṉṉar atu mūṭṭu valiyāka māṟi, itaya vālvukaḷ pātikkappaṭa vāyppu uṇṭu. ivvāṟu vaṟaṭṭu irumalil toṭaṅki, kāyccaluṭaṉ kūṭiya mūṭṭu valikku, rumāṭṭik kāyccal eṉṟu peyar. ulakam muḻuvatum rumāṭṭik kāyccalāl (rhematic fever) ēṟpaṭum vālvuk kōḷāṟukaḷ tām (Valvular problems) karppamuṟṟa peṇkaḷiṉ itaya nōykkāṉa mutaṉmi piracciṉikaḷākiṉṟaṉa.

aṟuvi cikicci avaciyamā? : vālvuk kōḷāṟukaḷ uḷḷa peṇkaḷ, maruttuvariṉ ālōcaṉippaṭi, muṟiyāka cikicci eṭuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. avvāṟu cikicci ceytu koṇṭāl, karppiṇikkum, piṟakka irukkum kuḻantikkum evvitap pātippum ēṟpaṭātu. mikat tīviramāṉa vālvu pātippuḷḷa peṇkaḷ tavira maṟṟavarkaḷiṉ karppa kālamum, piracava kālamum, maruttuvak kaṭṭuppāṭṭukkum, ārōkkiyattukkum uṭpaṭṭē irukkum. mēlum, vālvu pātippu tīviramāka illāviṭṭāl, aṟuvi cikicci tēviyilli. maruttu cikicciyē pōtumāṉatu.



﻿piṟavik kōḷāṟu : piṟavi itayak kōḷāṟukaḷāl pātikkappaṭṭuḷḷavarkaḷiṉ kōḷāṟic cari ceyya, taṟpōtu nalla cikicci muṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl ittakiyavarkaḷ kuḻantippēṟu aṭivatil cikkal ētum illi. itē pōṉṟu, itaya aṟuvi cikicci ceytu koṇṭavarkaḷum, ceyaṟki vālvu poruttappaṭṭavarkaḷum, kūṭa tirumaṇam muṭittu, makappēṟu aṭiyum vāyppu niccayam uṇṭu.

peṇkaḷum māraṭippum : peṇkaḷukku māraṭippu ēṟpaṭuvatu kuṟivu eṉṟa kaṇippu, ittaṉi nāḷ iruntu vantatu. inta nili ippōtu māṟi varukiṟatu. ēṉ? eppaṭi?

muṉṉar irunta vāḻkki muṟiyil, iyantira vēkamum, pataṟṟattit tarum kavalikaḷum, peṇkaḷukku ēṟpaṭuvatu kuṟivāka iruntatu. kūṭavē, peṇkaḷiṉ hārmōṉāṉa īsṭrōjaṉ, māraṭippu ēṟpaṭuvatiyum, iratta nāḷaṅkaḷ aṭipaṭuvatiyum, nāḷaṅkaḷil kolasṭirāl paṭivatiyum, taṭi ceykiṟatu. īsṭrōjaṉiṉ intac ceyalukku ‘kārṭiyō puroṭakṭṭiv eḵpekṭ’.” (Cardio protective effect), allatu itayap pātukāval tiṟaṉ eṉṟu peyar.

mātaviṭāy niṟkum vari : īsṭrōjaṉ kāraṇamāka, peṇkaḷukku māraṭippu ēṟpaṭum catavikitam kuṟivāka ikuttatu. iruppiṉum, mātaviṭāy niṉṟa piṟaku (meṉōpās), īsṭrōjaṉ curappu kuṟiyum niliyil, peṇkaḷukku māraṭippu ēṟpaṭum vāyppu atikirikkiṟatu. in niliyil āṇkaḷukkuc camamāṉa vikitattil, peṇkaḷum māraṭippāl pātikkappaṭum vāyppukaḷ uṇṭu.



﻿muṉpu mātaviṭāy niṉṟa piṟakē, māraṭippu ēṟpaṭa vāypput tēṉṟiyatu. taṟpōtiya paraparappu mikunta vāḻkkik cūḻal, uṭal parumaṉ, koḻuppu niṟinta uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷ kāraṇāka, mātaviṭāy niṟpataṟku muṉpēkūṭa māraṭippukku āḷākat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar.

māraṭippiṉ tīviram āṇkaḷiviṭa atikam : iṉṉoṉṟiyum niṉivil vittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. oru peṇṇukku māraṭippu ēṟpaṭṭāl, nōyiṉ tīviram āṇik kāṭṭilum atikam eṉṟu kaṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ēṉeṉil, peṇṇiṉ karōṉar tamaṉikaḷ (Coronary arteries), ciṟiyatākavum viṭṭattil kuṟivākavum uḷḷaṉa. eṉavē māraṭippu eṟpaṭṭu viṭṭāl, nōy pātippu atikamākavē uḷḷatu.

taṭuppu eṉṉa? : paraparappu illāta vāḻkki muṟi, 40 vayatu mutalē koḻuppuc cattu niṟinta ney uḷpaṭa, uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷik kuṟittuc cāppiṭutal, tiṉamum uṭaṟpayiṟci ceytu, uyarattukku ēṟṟa eṭiyip parāmarittal ākiyavi mūlam, itaya nōykaḷ varāmal taṭuttukkoḷḷa muṭiyum.





pōliyōvukku viṭi koṭuppōm

nam nāṭṭil periyammi, piḷēk pōṉṟa koṭiya nōykaḷi muṟṟilum oḻittatu pōla, pōliyō nōyiyum oḻikka, tīvira naṭavaṭikkikaḷ eṭukkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.

cukātārap piracciṉi : ki, kāli ūṉamākkum āṟṟal paṭitta pōliyō virassi, 2000-m āṇṭukkuḷ ulakattiliruntē aṟavē oḻikka vēṇṭum eṉa, 12 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu, ulaka cukātāra niṟuvaṉam ilakku nirṇayittatu. iti muṉṉiṭṭup pōliyō nōy oḻippu muṟikaḷ ulakam muḻuvatum tīvirappaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. 156-kkum mēṟpaṭṭa mēli nāṭukaḷ pōliyō nōyi oḻittu veṟṟi kaṇṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, intiyā, maṟṟum atic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa teṉkiḻakku āciya nāṭukaḷil, pōliyō oru cukātārap piracciṉiyāka iruntu varukiṟatu.

80 catavītak kuḻantikaḷ : ulakam muḻuvatum pōliyō nōyiṉāl pātikkappaṭṭa mottak kuḻantikaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyil, teṉkiḻakku āciya nāṭukaḷiṉ paṅku maṭṭum, 80 catavītam ākum. eṉavē, ﻿nāmum namatu aṇṭi nāṭukaḷum, pōliyō nōyi oḻittāl tāṉ pōliyō nōyiliruntu ulakam viṭupaṭa muṭiyum.

pōliyō viras paravuvatu eppaṭi? : pōliyō nōy parappum nuṇkirumikaḷ, perumpālum aintu vayatukku uṭpaṭṭa kuḻantikaḷēyē tākkukiṉṟaṉa. ik kirumikaḷ, malattiṉ mūlam paravukiṉṟaṉa. mūṭi vikkappaṭāmal, ī moytta uṇavu, kaḻippaṟi ceṉṟuviṭṭu, cōppu pōṭṭuk ki kaḻuvāmal cāppiṭutal, ākiyavi kāraṇamāka vāy vaḻiyākak kuḻantiyiṉ vayiṟṟukkuḷ ik kirumi celkiṟatu. piṉṉar kuṭalil taṅki pala maṭaṅkākap perukukiṟatu.

ūṉam ēṉ?: kuṭalil palkip perukum pōliyōk kirumikaḷ, ratta oṭṭattil kalantu, mukutut taṇṭu vaṭattil uḷḷa narampu maṇṭalattit tākkat toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu, narampu maṇṭalam pātikkappaṭuvatāl, tacip pakutikaḷ palavīṉam aṭikiṉṟaṉa. kuḻantikkuk kāyccal ēṟpaṭṭu, ki, kālkaḷ tuvaḷat toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. nōy tīviramaṭiyum niliyil, ki allatu kālil nirantara ūṉam ēṟpaṭukiṟatu.

kuḻantatikaḷukku ēṉ : kuḻantikaḷukku, aintu vayatu āvataṟkuḷ mūḷi uḷpaṭa, narampil toṭarpuṭiya iṇippukaḷ, 90 catavīta vaḷarcci aṭikiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu vaḷarcci aṭiyum niliyil,pōliyō viras mutukut taṇṭuvaṭattit tākkumpōtu perumpālum aintu vayatukkuṭpaṭṭa kuḻantikaḷ pātippukku uḷḷākiṉṟaṉar.

100-l oru kuḻanti : pōliyō viras tākkiṉālum, ellāk kuḻantikaḷum ūṉam aṭivatilli. 100 kuḻantikaḷip pōliyō viras ﻿tākkiṉāl, oru kuḻantikku ūṉam ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. nōy etirppuc cakti kāraṇamāka, maṟṟa kuḻantikaḷukkup pātippu ēṟpaṭāta kuḻantikaḷiṉ, kuṭalil pōliyō viras taṅki irukkum.

veḷiyēṟṟuvatu eppaṭi? : ūṉamō allatu vēṟu viḷivukaḷō ēṟpaṭāmal uḷḷa kuḻantikaḷiṉ uṭalil iruntu pōliyō virasi malam mūlam veḷiyēṟṟa muṭiyum. itaṟkākavē ilavacamāka pōliyō coṭṭu maruntu koṭukkappaṭukiṟatu. aṉittuk kuḻantikaḷukkum pōliyō coṭṭu marunti orē nērattil koṭuppataṉ mūlam kirumikaḷ aṉittum orē nērattil malattiṉ mūlam veḷiyēṟṟappaṭum. ivvāṟu veḷiyēṟum kirumikaḷ cuṟṟuppuṟa veppattil 48 maṇi nērattukku mēl uyirvāḻa muṭiyāmal maṭintu viṭum. nōy parappum pōliyō viras kirumikaḷ entak kuḻantiyiṉ kuṭalilum taṅkip peruka vāyppu irukkātu. eṉavē tāṉ entak kuḻantikkum viṭṭup pōkāmal coṭṭu maruntu koṭuppataṟkāṉa tīvira muyaṟcikaḷ eṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṟku peṟṟōriṉ ottuḻippu mika avaciyam.

tamiḻakattil : ovvoru kuḻantikkum taṭuppu maruntukaḷ aṭṭavaṇippaṭi piṟantu oṉṟari vayatu āvataṟkuḷ āṟu muṟi pōliyō coṭṭu maruntu koṭukkappaṭukiṟatu. ivvāṟu koṭuttum kūṭa pōliyō viras cuṟṟuppuṟattil iruntatāl kaṭanta aintu āṇṭukaḷākat toṭarntu tīvira ciṟappu pōliyō coṭṭu maruntu mukāmkaḷ naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

ivvāṟu tīvira ciṟappu coṭṭu maruntu mukāmkaḷ naṭattiyataṉ mūlam tamiḻakattil palaṉ kiṭittuḷḷatu. 10 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu tamiḻakattil ovvōr āṇṭum 5 ﻿āyirattukkum mēṟpaṭṭa kuḻantikaḷ pōliyō nōyiṉāl pātikkapaṭum nili iruntatu. tīvira ciṟappu coṭṭu maruntu mukāmkaḷ kāraṇamāka 1999-l tamiḻakattil 11 kuḻantikaḷ pōliyō virasāl pātikkappaṭṭaṉar; ivarkaḷil tūttukkuṭiyic cērnta iraṇṭu kuḻantikaḷ uḷpaṭa mūṉṟu kuḻantikaḷ maṭṭumē ūṉam aṭiyum aḷavukkup pātikkappaṭṭaṉar. inta aḷavukku eṇṇikki vekuvākak kuṟintuḷḷatu. eṉavē virasi cuṟṟuppuṟattiliruntu oḻippataṉ mūlam pōliyō nōy oḻippip pūrtti ceyya muṭiyum.

nōy varāmal taṭuttukkoḷḷa eṉṉa vaḻi ? : kuḻanti piṟantatu mutalē aṭṭavaṇippaṭi pōliyō coṭṭu maruntu koṭuttal, ciṟappu mukāmkaḷ naṭattappaṭum pōtum coṭṭu maruntu koṭuttal ākiyavi mūlam pōliyō tākkutalil iruntu kuḻantiyik kākka muṭiyum. mēlum kuḻanti vaḷarum mutal 5 āṇṭutāṉ mukkiyamāṉa vaḷarccip paruvam eṉpatāl purataccattu niṟinta uṇavukaḷit toṭarntu koṭuppatu avaciyam. pāl, muṭṭi, mīṉ, paccik kāykaṟikaḷi uṇavil cērttuk koṭuppatu avaciyam. ēṉeṉil pōliyō nōykut taṭuppu uṇṭu. cikicci kiṭiyātu. nōy vanta piṟaku ūṭṭaccattu uṇavukaḷ koṭuttup palaṉ illi.

peṟṟōrē : kaṭanta akṭōpar, navamparil naṭanta mutal iraṇṭu tavaṇi pōliyō coṭṭu maruntu mukāmkaḷil peṟṟōriṉ muḻu ottuḻippu kāraṇamāka tamiḻakattil 70 laṭcam kuḻantikaḷukku coṭṭu maruntu koṭukkappaṭṭatu. eṉavē varum ṭica.19 (ñāyiṟu), jaṉavari 23 (ñāyiṟu) ākiya iraṇṭu tētikaḷilum naṭipeṟum ciṟappu pōliyō coṭṭu maruntu mukāmkaḷil atē ottuḻippit tara peṟṟōr tavaṟak kūṭātu.





inta miṉṉūlip paṟṟi


uṅkaḷukku immiṉṉūl, iṇiya nūlakamāṉa, vikkimūlattil iruntu kiṭittuḷḷatu[1].

inta iṇiya nūlakam taṉṉārvalarkaḷāl vaḷarukiṟatu. vikkimūlam patiya taṉṉārvalarkaḷi varavēṟkiṟatu. tāṅkaḷum vikkimūlattil iṇintu mēlum pala miṉṉūlkaḷi aṉivarum paṭikkumāṟu ceyyalām.

mikunta akkaṟiyuṭaṉ meyppu ceytālum, miṉṉūlil piḻi ētēṉum iruntāl tayakkam illāmal, vikkimūlattil immiṉṉūliṉ pēccu pakkattil terivikkalām allatu piḻikaḷi nīṅkaḷē kūṭa cari ceyyalām.

ippaṭippākkam, kaṭṭaṟṟa urimaṅkaḷōṭu (potukaḷa /kuṉu -Commons /GNU FDL )[2][3] ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, inta uriyi nīṅkaḷ maṟṟavarōṭu pakiralām; māṟṟi mēmpaṭuttalām; vaṇika nōkkattōṭum, vaṇika nōkkamiṉṟiyum payaṉpaṭuttalām

immiṉṉūl cāttiyamāvataṟku paṅkaḷittavarkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu:

Deepa arul kaniyam

Kaleeswari kaniyam

Arularasan. G

Info-farmer

Thiyagu Ganesh

Rabiyathul

NithyaSathiyaraj

HoboJones

Fleshgrinder

Be..anyone

Patricknoddy~commonswiki

Rocket000

Xato

Mecredis
